<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 354. TEACHERS RETIREMENT</h2>
      
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.01"><b>354.01</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/83A.01">83A.01</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.02"><b>354.02</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/83A.02">83A.02</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.03"><b>354.03</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/83A.03">83A.03</a>]
</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.04"><b>354.04</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/83A.04">83A.04</a>]
</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.045">
        <h1 class="shn">354.045 CITATION.</h1>
        <p>This chapter may be cited as the "Teachers Retirement Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.045.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/485/#laws.0.39.0">1969 c 485 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.1.0">1974 c 289 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.05">
        <h1 class="shn">354.05 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Terms.</span></h2>
          <p>Unless the language or context clearly indicates that a different meaning is intended, the following terms, for the purposes of this chapter, shall be given the meanings subjoined to them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Teacher.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Teacher" means:</p>
          <p>(1) a person who renders service as a teacher, supervisor, principal, superintendent, librarian, nurse, counselor, social worker, therapist, or psychologist in:</p>
          <p>(i) a public school of the state other than in Independent School District No. 625;</p>
          <p>(ii) a charter school; or</p>
          <p>(iii) the Perpich Center for Arts Education, except that any employee of the Perpich Center for Arts Education who was covered by the Minnesota State Retirement System general state employees retirement plan as of July 1, 2018, shall continue to be covered by that plan and not by the Teachers Retirement Association;</p>
          <p>(2) a person who is engaged in educational administration in connection with the state public school system, whether the position be a public office or as employment;</p>
          <p>(3) a person who renders service as a charter school director or chief administrative officer; provided, however, that if the charter school director or chief administrative officer is covered by the Public Employees Retirement Association general employees retirement plan on July 1, 2018, the charter school director or chief administrative officer shall continue to be covered by that plan and not by the Teachers Retirement Association;</p>
          <p>(4) an employee of the Teachers Retirement Association;</p>
          <p>(5) a person who renders teaching service on a part-time basis and who also renders other services for a single employing unit where the teaching service comprises at least 50 percent of the combined employment salary is a member of the association for all services with the single employing unit or, if less than 50 percent of the combined employment salary, the executive director determines all of the combined service is covered by the association; or</p>
          <p>(6) a person who is not covered by the plans established under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352D">352D</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A">354A</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354B">354B</a> and who is employed by the Board of Trustees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system in an unclassified position as:</p>
          <p>(i) a president, vice-president, or dean;</p>
          <p>(ii) a manager or a professional in an academic or an academic support program other than specified in item (i);</p>
          <p>(iii) an administrative or a service support faculty position; or</p>
          <p>(iv) a teacher or a research assistant.</p>
          <p>(b) "Teacher" does not mean:</p>
          <p>(1) a person who works for a school or institution as an independent contractor as defined by the Internal Revenue Service;</p>
          <p>(2) annuitants of the teachers retirement plan who are employed after retirement by an employing unit that participates in the teachers retirement plan during the course of that reemployment;</p>
          <p>(3) a person who is employed by the University of Minnesota;</p>
          <p>(4) a member or an officer of any general governing or managing board or body of an employing unit that participates in the teachers retirement plan; or</p>
          <p>(5) a person employed by Independent School District No. 625 as a teacher as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A.011#stat.354A.011.27">354A.011, subdivision 27</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivision 2, a person specified in paragraph (b) is not a member of the association except for purposes of Social Security coverage unless the person is covered by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354B.211">354B.211</a>, and elects coverage by the Teachers Retirement Association.</p>
          <p>(b) A teacher is excluded from association membership other than Social Security coverage under paragraph (a) if the person is covered by the individual retirement account plan established under chapter 354B.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Teaching.</span></h2>
          <p>The word "teaching" includes the service performed by any person coming within the definition of "teacher" as set forth in subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Fund.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "fund" means the teachers retirement fund referred to in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Member of the association.</span></h2>
          <p>"Member of the association" means every teacher who contributes to the teachers retirement fund as provided in this chapter who has not retired, or a teacher who exercises an option to elect coverage under another public pension plan enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>. Any former member of the association who is retired and subsequently resumes teaching service is a member of the association only for purposes of Social Security coverage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Board.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "board" means the board of trustees of the Teachers Retirement Association.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Actuarial equivalent.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Actuarial equivalent" means the condition of one annuity or benefit having an equal actuarial present value as another annuity or benefit, determined as of a given date with each actuarial present value based on the appropriate mortality table adopted by the board of trustees based on the experience of the association as recommended by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>, and approved under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.18">356.215, subdivision 18</a>, and using the applicable investment return assumption specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of computing a joint and survivor annuity, the investment return assumption specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.461">356.461</a> must be used rather than the investment return specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Dependent child.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dependent child" means a biological or adopted child of a deceased member who has not reached the age of 18, or who is under age 22 and is a full-time student throughout the normal school year, unmarried and dependent for more than one-half of support upon the member. It also means a child of the member conceived during the member's lifetime and born after the member's death.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.8a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.8a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8a.<span class="headnote">Dependent child; alternative definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purpose of survivor benefit eligibility under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.46#stat.354.46.2b">354.46, subdivision 2b</a>, "dependent child" means any biological or adopted child of a deceased member who has not reached the age of 20 and is dependent for more than one-half of support upon the member. It also includes any child of the member conceived while living and born after death.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Association.</span></h2>
          <p>"Association" means the combined membership of all teachers who qualify and participate in the retirement program provided for in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Approved actuary.</span></h2>
          <p>"Approved actuary" means an actuary who meets the definition in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.1">356.215, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Accumulated deductions.</span></h2>
          <p>"Accumulated deductions" means the total of the sums deducted from the salary of a member and the total amount of assessments paid by a member in lieu of such deductions, credited to the member's individual fund, less amounts paid to the member or any person in the member's behalf in the form of refundments, annuity payments or benefit payments and less any other amounts deducted pursuant to law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Payments in lieu of deductions.</span></h2>
          <p>"Payments in lieu of deductions" means the assessments or payments made by any member to receive credit for service rendered when no salary deductions were made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Allowable service.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Allowable service" means:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) any service rendered by a teacher for any calendar month when the member receives salary from which deductions are made, deposited and credited in the fund;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) any service rendered by a person for any calendar month where payments in lieu of salary deductions are made, deposited and credited into the fund as provided in Minnesota Statutes 1980, section 354.09, subdivision 4, and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.53">354.53</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) any service rendered by a teacher for which the teacher elected to obtain credit for service by making payments to the fund under Minnesota Statutes 1980, section 354.09, subdivisions 1 and 4, sections 354.50, 354.51, Minnesota Statutes 1957, section 135.41, subdivision 4, Minnesota Statutes 1971, section 354.09, subdivision 2, or Minnesota Statutes, 1973 Supplement, section 354.09, subdivision 3;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) both service during years of actual membership in the course of which contributions were currently made and service in years during which the teacher was not a member but for which the teacher later elected to obtain credit by making payments to the fund as permitted by any law then in effect;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) any service rendered where contributions were made and no credit was established because of the limitations contained in Minnesota Statutes 1957, section 135.09, subdivision 2, as determined by the ratio between the amounts of money credited to the teacher's account in a fiscal year and the maximum retirement contribution allowable for that year;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) a period of time during which a teacher was on strike without pay, not to exceed a period of one year, if payment in lieu of salary deductions is made under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.72">354.72</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) a period of service before July 1, 2006, that was properly credited as allowable service by the Minneapolis Teachers Retirement Fund Association, and that was rendered by a teacher as an employee of Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, or by an employee of the Minneapolis Teachers Retirement Fund Association who was a member of the Minneapolis Teachers Retirement Fund Association by virtue of that employment, who has not begun receiving an annuity or other retirement benefit from the former Minneapolis Teachers Retirement Fund Association calculated in whole or in part on that service before July 1, 2006, and who has not taken a refund of member contributions related to that service unless the refund is repaid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.50">354.50</a>, subdivision 4. Service as an employee of Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, on or after July 1, 2006, is "allowable service" only as provided by this chapter; or</p>
          <p>(8) a period of service before July 1, 2015, that was properly credited as allowable service by the Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association, and that was rendered by a teacher as an employee of Independent School District No. 709, Duluth, or by an employee of the Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association who was a member of the Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association by virtue of that employment, who has not begun receiving an annuity or other retirement benefit from the former Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association calculated in whole or in part on that service before July 1, 2015, and who has not taken a refund of member contributions related to that service unless the refund is repaid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.50">354.50</a>, subdivision 4. Service as an employee of Independent School District No. 709, Duluth, on or after July 1, 2015, is "allowable service" only as provided by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.13a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.13a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13a.<span class="headnote">Average salary.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Average salary," for the purpose of determining the member's retirement annuity, means the average salary upon which contributions were made for the highest five successive years of formula service credit.</p>
          <p>(b) "Average salary" may not include any more than the equivalent of 60 monthly salary payments.</p>
          <p>(c) "Average salary" must be based upon all years of formula service credit if this service credit is less than five years.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.14">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.14">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Total and permanent disability.</span></h2>
          <p>"Total and permanent disability" means the inability to engage in any substantial gainful activity by reason of any medically determinable physical or mental impairment which can be expected to be of long continued and indefinite duration. An "indefinite duration" is a period of at least one year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.14a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.14a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14a.<span class="headnote">Surviving spouse.</span></h2>
          <p>"Surviving spouse" means the spouse of a deceased member or a disabilitant who was legally married to the member at the time of death.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.15">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 15." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.15">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.3.34.0">1994 c 528 art 3 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.16">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 16." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.16">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Retirement annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>"Retirement annuity" means the payments made by the association to a former member after retirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.17">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.17">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Optional survivors annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>"Optional survivors annuity" means the payments made by the association to a survivor of a former member pursuant to an optional annuity form selected by such member at or before retirement which varies in amount depending primarily upon both employee and employer contributions made by and in behalf of the particular member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.17a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.17a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17a.<span class="headnote">Former spouse.</span></h2>
          <p>"Former spouse" means a person who is no longer a spouse of a member due to dissolution of the marriage, legal separation, or annulment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.18">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 18." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.18">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.19">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 19." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.19">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>"Annuity" means a retirement annuity, optional survivors annuity, or spouses annuity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.20">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 20." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.20">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Benefit.</span></h2>
          <p>"Benefit" means an allowance paid or payable by the association to a surviving dependent spouse or a dependent child which is a fixed amount and also includes an allowance paid or payable by the association to a member or former member who is permanently and totally disabled.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.21">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 21." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.21">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Retirement.</span></h2>
          <p>"Retirement" means the withdrawal of a member from active teaching service who is paid a retirement annuity thereafter and commences with the date designated by the retirement board when the retirement annuity first accrues to the former member after withdrawal from active teaching service and application for an annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44">354.44</a>, subdivisions 3 and 4. The effective date of retirement must occur for an annuity plan selection to take effect. This date determines any rights specified in this chapter which occur either before or after retirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.22">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 22." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.22">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Designated beneficiary.</span></h2>
          <p>"Designated beneficiary" means the person, trust, or organization designated by a retiree or member to receive the benefits to which a beneficiary is entitled under this chapter. A beneficiary designation is valid only if it is made on an appropriate form provided by the executive director that is signed by the member. The properly completed form must be received by the association on or before the date of death of the retiree or member. If a retiree or a member does not designate a person, trust, or organization, or if the person who was designated predeceases the retiree or the member, or if the trust or organization ceases to exist before the death of the retiree or the member, the designated beneficiary is the estate of the deceased retiree or member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.23">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 23." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.23">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.64.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 64</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.24">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 24." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.24">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.64.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 64</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.25">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 25." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.25">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.<span class="headnote">Formula service credit.</span></h2>
          <p>"Formula service credit" means any allowable service credit as defined in subdivision 13 except:</p>
          <p>(1) any service rendered for which contributions were not made in full as determined by the ratio between the amounts of money credited to the teacher's account in a fiscal year and the retirement contribution payable for the fiscal year under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.092">354.092</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.42">354.42</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.51">354.51</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) no period of service may be counted more than once for purposes of this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.26">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 26." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.26">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.77.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 77</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.27">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 27." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.27">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.<span class="headnote">Normal school operating funds.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "normal school operating funds" as used in this chapter shall be defined as and limited to the proceeds of property tax levies, state school maintenance cost aids distributed in accordance with statutes governing such funds, state aid to distressed school districts, proceeds from federal forest reserve lands, state transportation aids, and receipts from tuition paid by persons or other school districts and rental charges received. The term also includes money appropriated by the legislature for the operation of any state agency, department, state board, or institution.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.28">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 28." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.28">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 28.<span class="headnote">Satisfactory certification.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "satisfactory certification" means a certification executed by a responsible officer of an employing authority in such form and content that false or misleading information would constitute perjury.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.29">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 29." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.29">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.3.34.0">1994 c 528 art 3 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.30">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 30." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.30">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.<span class="headnote">Coordinated member.</span></h2>
          <p>"Coordinated member" means any teacher covered by any agreement or modification made between the state and the Secretary of Health, Education and Welfare, making the provisions of the federal Old Age, Survivors and Disability Insurance Act applicable to such member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.31">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 31." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.31">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.<span class="headnote">Basic member.</span></h2>
          <p>"Basic member" means any teacher not covered by any agreement or modification made between the state and the Secretary of Health, Education and Welfare.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.32">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 32." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.32">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.<span class="headnote">Formula program.</span></h2>
          <p>"Formula program" means a retirement program which provides benefits based on certain percentages multiplied by the years of service and average salary of a member as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44">354.44</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.33">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 33." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.33">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.64.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 64</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.34">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 34." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.34">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.64.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 64</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.35">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 35." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.35">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.<span class="headnote">Salary.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Subject to the limitations of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611">356.611</a>, "salary" means the periodic compensation, upon which member contributions are required before deductions for deferred compensation, supplemental retirement plans, or other voluntary salary reduction programs.</p>
          <p>(b) "Salary" does not mean:</p>
          <p>(1) lump-sum annual leave payments;</p>
          <p>(2) lump-sum wellness and sick leave payments;</p>
          <p>(3) employer-paid amounts used by an employee toward the cost of insurance coverage, employer-paid fringe benefits, flexible spending accounts, cafeteria plans, health care expense accounts, day care expenses, or any payments in lieu of any employer-paid group insurance coverage, including the difference between single and family rates that may be paid to a member with single coverage and certain amounts determined by the executive director to be ineligible;</p>
          <p>(4) any form of payment made in lieu of any other employer-paid fringe benefit or expense;</p>
          <p>(5) any form of severance payments;</p>
          <p>(6) workers' compensation payments;</p>
          <p>(7) disability insurance payments, including self-insured disability payments;</p>
          <p>(8) payments to school principals and all other administrators for services that are in addition to the normal work year contract if these additional services are performed on an extended duty day, Saturday, Sunday, holiday, annual leave day, sick leave day, or any other nonduty day;</p>
          <p>(9) payments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.24#stat.356.24.1">356.24, subdivision 1</a>, clause (4); and</p>
          <p>(10) payments made under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.12">122A.40, subdivision 12</a>, except for payments for sick leave that are accumulated under the provisions of a uniform school district policy that applies equally to all similarly situated persons in the district.</p>
          <p>(c) Amounts provided to an employee by the employer through a grievance proceeding or a legal settlement are salary only if the settlement is reviewed by the executive director and the amounts are determined by the executive director to be consistent with paragraph (a) and prior determinations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.35a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 35a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.35a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35a.<span class="headnote">Severance payments.</span></h2>
          <p>Severance payments include, but are not limited to:</p>
          <p>(1) payments to an employee to terminate employment;</p>
          <p>(2) payments, or that portion of payments, that are not clearly for the performance of services by the employee to the employer;</p>
          <p>(3) payments to an administrator or former administrator serving as an advisor to a successor or as a consultant to the employer under an agreement to terminate employment within two years or less of the execution of the agreement for compensation that is significantly different than the most recent contract salary;</p>
          <p>(4) payments under a procedure that allows the employee to designate the time of payment if the payments are made during the period of formula service credit used to compute a benefit or annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a> or 7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.46#stat.354.46.1">354.46, subdivision 1</a> or 2; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.48#stat.354.48.3">354.48, subdivision 3</a>; and</p>
          <p>(5) lump-sum payments during the time period that contains the highest five successive years of salary for additional services performed without pay during other years of salary.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.36">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 36." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.36">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.<span class="headnote">Retiree of association.</span></h2>
          <p>"Retiree of association" means any former member who has retired as provided in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.37">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 37." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.37">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.<span class="headnote">Termination of teaching service.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Termination of teaching service" means the withdrawal of a member from active teaching service by resignation or the termination of the member's teaching contract by the employer. A member is not considered to have terminated teaching service, if before the age of 62, and before the effective date of the termination or retirement, the member has entered into a contract to resume teaching service with an employing unit covered by the provisions of this chapter. A contract to return to work after retirement for an active member who has attained age 62 must comply with the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.444">354.444</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.38">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 38." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.38">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.<span class="headnote">Normal retirement age.</span></h2>
          <p>"Normal retirement age" means age 65 for a person who first became a member of the association or a member of a pension fund listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, before July 1, 1989. Through June 30, 2024, for a person who first becomes a member of the association after June 30, 1989, normal retirement age means the higher of age 65 or "retirement age," as defined in United States Code, title 42, section 416(l), as amended, but not to exceed age 66. Beginning July 1, 2024, normal retirement age for all members means age 65.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.39">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 39." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.39">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.<span class="headnote">Administrator's normal work year.</span></h2>
          <p>Normal work year for school principals and all other administrators means 12 consecutive full months of continuous employment from July 1 to June 30.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.40">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 40." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.40">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Timely receipt.</span></h2>
          <p>An application, payment, return, claim, or other document that is not personally delivered to the association on or before the applicable due date is considered to be a timely receipt if received on or before the due date or if delivered or filed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/645.151">645.151</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.41">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 41." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.41">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 41.<span class="headnote">Annual base salary.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Annual base salary" means:</p>
          <p>(1) for an independent school district or educational cooperative, the lowest full-time Bachelor of Arts (BA) base contract salary for the previous fiscal year for that employing unit;</p>
          <p>(2) for a charter school, the lowest starting annual salary for a full-time teacher employed during the previous fiscal year for that employing unit; and</p>
          <p>(3) for a state agency or professional organization, the lowest starting annual salary for a full-time Teachers Retirement Association covered position for the previous fiscal year for that employing unit.</p>
          <p>(b) If there is no previous fiscal year data because an employer unit is new and paragraph (c) does not apply, the annual base salary for the first year of operation will be as provided in paragraph (a), except that the base contract salary for the current fiscal year, rather than the previous fiscal year, must be used.</p>
          <p>(c) For a new employer unit created as a result of a merger or consolidation, the annual base salary must be the lowest annual base salary as specified in paragraph (a) for any of the employer units involved in the merger or consolidation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.42">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 42." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.42">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 42.<span class="headnote">Fiscal year.</span></h2>
          <p>The fiscal year of the association begins on July 1 of each calendar year and ends on June 30 of the following calendar year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.05.43">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 43." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.05.43">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 43.<span class="headnote">APRN.</span></h2>
          <p>"APRN" means an individual licensed as an advanced practice registered nurse by the Board of Nursing as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.171#stat.148.171.3">148.171, subdivision 3</a>, who provides services to a member or an applicant that are within the scope of the APRN's professional licensure.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.354.06">
        <h1 class="shn">354.06 BOARD OF TRUSTEES; MEMBERSHIP; DUTIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.06.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.06.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Board.</span></h2>
          <p>The management of the association is vested in a board of eight trustees known as the board of trustees of the Teachers Retirement Association. It is composed of the following persons: the commissioner of education, the commissioner of management and budget, a representative of the Minnesota School Boards Association, four members of the association elected by the members of the association, and one retiree elected by the retirees of the association. The five elected members of the board of trustees must be chosen by ballot in a manner fixed by the board of trustees of the association. In every odd-numbered year there shall be elected two members of the association to the board of trustees for terms of four years commencing on the first of July next succeeding their election. In every other odd-numbered year one retiree of the association must be elected to the board of trustees for a term of four years commencing on the first of July next succeeding the election. The filing of candidacy for a retiree election must include a petition of endorsement signed by at least ten retirees of the association. Each election must be completed by June first of each succeeding odd-numbered year. In the case of elective members, any vacancy must be filled by appointment by the remainder of the board, and the appointee shall serve until the members or retirees of the association at the next regular election have elected a trustee to serve for the unexpired term caused by the vacancy. No member or retiree may be appointed by the board, or elected by the members of the association as a trustee, if the person is not a member or retiree of the association in good standing at the time of the appointment or election.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.06.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.06.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Fiduciary duty.</span></h2>
          <p>It is the duty of the board of trustees and the executive director to undertake their activities consistent with chapter 356A.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.06.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.06.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">President; executive director.</span></h2>
          <p>The board must annually elect one of its members as president. It must elect an executive director. Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, the board must set the salary of the executive director. The board must review the performance of the executive director on an annual basis and may grant salary adjustments as a result of the review. The executive director must serve at the pleasure of the board and be the executive officer of the board, with the duties prescribed in subdivision 2a and any additional duties that the board may prescribe. The board must employ all other clerks and employees necessary to properly administer the association. The board must appoint an executive director on the basis of education, experience in the retirement field, ability to manage and lead system staff, and ability to assist the board in setting a vision for the system. The executive director must have had at least five years of experience in either an executive-level management position or in a position with responsibility for the governance, management, or administration of a retirement plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.06.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.06.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Duties of executive director.</span></h2>
          <p>The management of the association is vested in the executive director who shall be the executive and administrative head of the association. The executive director shall act as advisor to the board on all matters pertaining to the association and shall also act as the secretary of the board. The executive director shall:</p>
          <p>(1) attend all meetings of the board;</p>
          <p>(2) prepare and recommend to the board appropriate rules to carry out the provisions of this chapter;</p>
          <p>(3) establish and maintain an adequate system of records and accounts following recognized accounting principles and controls;</p>
          <p>(4) designate, as necessary, a deputy executive director and an assistant executive director in the unclassified service, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.08">43A.08</a>, whose salaries shall be set in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.18#stat.43A.18.3">43A.18, subdivision 3</a>, and two assistant executive directors in the classified service, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.07">43A.07</a>, with the approval of the board, and appoint such employees, both permanent and temporary, as are necessary to carry out the provisions of this chapter;</p>
          <p>(5) organize the work of the association as the director deems necessary to fulfill the functions of the association, and define the duties of its employees and delegate to them any powers or duties, subject to the director's control and under such conditions as the director may prescribe;</p>
          <p>(6) with the approval of the board, contract and set the compensation for the services of an approved actuary, professional management services, and any other consulting services. These contracts are not subject to the competitive bidding procedure prescribed by chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16C">16C</a>. An approved actuary retained by the executive director shall function as the actuarial advisor of the board and the executive director and may perform actuarial valuations and experience studies to supplement those performed by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>. Any supplemental actuarial valuations or experience studies shall be filed with the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement. Copies of professional management survey reports must be transmitted to the secretary of the senate, the chief clerk of the house of representatives, and the Legislative Reference Library as provided by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.195">3.195</a>, and to the executive director of the commission at the same time as reports are furnished to the board. Only management firms experienced in conducting management surveys of federal, state, or local public retirement systems are qualified to contract with the executive director;</p>
          <p>(7) with the approval of the board, provide in-service training for the employees of the association;</p>
          <p>(8) make refunds of accumulated contributions to former members and to the designated beneficiary, surviving spouse, legal representative, or next of kin of deceased members or deceased former members, under this chapter;</p>
          <p>(9) determine the amount of the annuities and disability benefits of members covered by the association and authorize payment of the annuities and benefits beginning as of the dates on which the annuities and benefits begin to accrue, under this chapter;</p>
          <p>(10) pay annuities, refunds, survivor benefits, salaries, and necessary operating expenses of the association;</p>
          <p>(11) prepare and submit to the board and the legislature an annual financial report covering the operation of the association, as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a>;</p>
          <p>(12) certify funds available for investment to the State Board of Investment;</p>
          <p>(13) with the advice and approval of the board, request the State Board of Investment to sell securities on determining that funds are needed for the purposes of the association;</p>
          <p>(14) prepare and submit biennial and annual budgets to the board and with the approval of the board submit those budgets to the Department of Management and Budget; and</p>
          <p>(15) with the approval of the board, perform such other duties as may be required for the administration of the association and the other provisions of this chapter and for the transaction of its business. The executive director may:</p>
          <p>(i) reduce all or part of the accrued interest and fines payable by an employing unit for reporting requirements under this chapter, based on an evaluation of any extenuating circumstances of the employing unit;</p>
          <p>(ii) assign association employees to conduct field audits of an employing unit to ensure compliance with the provisions of this chapter; and</p>
          <p>(iii) recover overpayments, if not repaid to the association, by suspending or reducing the payment of a retirement annuity, refund, disability benefit, survivor benefit, or optional annuity under this chapter until the overpayment, plus interest, has been recovered.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.06.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.06.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Commissioner of management and budget.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of management and budget shall be ex officio treasurer of the association and the commissioner's general bond to the state shall cover any liabilities for acts as treasurer of the association. The commissioner shall receive all moneys payable to the association and pay out the same only upon forms signed by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.06.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.06.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Board; expenses.</span></h2>
          <p>All members of the board shall serve without compensation. A member shall receive necessary expenses to attend meetings of the board and its committees, and association functions and presentations authorized by the board. The necessary expenses must be paid out of the fund. Members of the board shall suffer no loss of compensation from their employing units by reason of service on or for the association, the board, or any committee authorized by the board. Necessary expenses may include the salary of any substitute teacher which the employing unit is required to hire in the absence of the board member. The board may reimburse the employing unit for the cost of the substitute teacher.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.06.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.06.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Meetings.</span></h2>
          <p>The board shall meet regularly at its office at such times as it shall determine. Special meetings may be held at any time at the call of the president of the board or of any three members thereof.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.06.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.06.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.4.51.0">2009 c 169 art 4 s 51</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.06.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.06.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Office.</span></h2>
          <p>A suitable office shall be provided by the state through the proper officer for the use of the board and its executive director.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.06.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>

<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/47/#laws.5.1.0">2023 c 47 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.10.2.0">2024 c 102 art 10 s 2</a></p>

      <div class="section" id="stat.354.07">
        <h1 class="shn">354.07 POWERS OF THE BOARD.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.07.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.07.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">General powers of board.</span></h2>
          <p>The board has the power to frame bylaws for its own government and for the management of the association not inconsistent with the laws of the state and to modify them at its pleasure; to adopt, alter, and enforce reasonable rules not inconsistent with the laws of the state for the administration and management of the association, for the payment and collection of payments from members, and for the payment of withdrawals and benefits; to pass upon and allow or disallow applications for membership in the association and for credit for teaching service; to pass upon and allow or disallow claims for withdrawals, pensions, or benefits payable by the fund; to adopt an appropriate mortality table based on experience of the association as recommended by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214 </a>and using the applicable investment return assumption specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>; to approve early retirement and optional annuity factors, subject to review by the actuary retained by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement; to establish the schedule for implementation of the approved factors; to notify the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement of the implementation schedule; and to provide for the payment out of the fund of necessary expenses for the administration by the association and of claims for withdrawals, pensions, or benefits allowed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.07.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.07.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Investigatory powers.</span></h2>
          <p>In passing upon all applications and claims, the board may summon, swear, hear, and examine witnesses and, in the case of claims for disability benefits, may require the claimant to submit to a medical examination by a physician of the board's choice, at the expense of the claimant, as a condition precedent to the passing on the claim, and, in the case of all applications and claims, may conduct investigations necessary to determine the validity and merit of the same.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.07.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.07.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Attorney general; venue.</span></h2>
          <p>The attorney general shall be legal advisor to the board and the executive director. The board may sue or be sued or petitioned under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.96">356.96</a> in the name of the board of trustees of the Teachers Retirement Association. In all actions brought by or against it the board shall be represented by the attorney general. Except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.96#stat.356.96.13">356.96, subdivision 13</a>, venue of all actions is in the Ramsey County District Court.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.07.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.07.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Certification of funds to State Board of Investment.</span></h2>
          <p>It is the duty of the board from time to time to certify to the State Board of Investment for investment as much of the funds in its hands as shall not be needed for current purposes. The State Board of Investment shall thereupon invest and reinvest an amount equal to the sum so certified in such securities as are now or may hereafter be duly authorized legal investments for state employees retirement fund and all such securities so transferred or purchased must be deposited with the commissioner of management and budget. All interest from these investments must be credited to the teachers retirement fund and used for current purposes or investments, except as hereinafter provided. The State Board of Investment has authority to sell, convey, and exchange such securities and invest and reinvest the funds when it deems it desirable to do so, and must sell securities upon request of the officers of the association when such officers determine funds are needed for its purposes. All of the provisions regarding accounting procedures and restrictions and conditions for the purchase and sale of securities under chapter 11A must apply to the accounting, purchase and sale of securities for the Teachers' Retirement Association.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.07.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.07.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Records; accounts; interest.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board shall keep a record of the receipts and disbursements of the fund and a separate account for each member of the association. The board shall also keep separate accounts for annuity payments, for employer contributions and all other necessary accounts and reserves.</p>
          <p>(b) It shall determine annually the annual interest earnings of the fund which shall include realized capital gains and losses.</p>
          <p>(c) The annual interest earnings must be apportioned and credited to the separate members' accounts except those covered under the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>. The rate to be used in this distribution, computed to the last full quarter percent, must be determined by dividing the interest earnings by the total invested assets of the fund. The excess of the annual interest earnings in the excess earnings reserve which was not credited to the various accounts must be credited to the gross interest earnings for the next succeeding year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.07.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.07.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/485/#laws.0.40.0">1969 c 485 s 40</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.07.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.07.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Electronic data processing.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding chapter 16B or 16C or any law to the contrary, the board may use the services of the Department of Administration, Information Services Division, for electronic data processing and related services or may contract for all or a portion of such services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.07.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.07.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/564/#laws.0.51.0">1984 c 564 s 51</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.07.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.07.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Information distribution.</span></h2>
          <p>All school districts, the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities, and other employers of members of the association are obligated to distribute to their employees ballots for the election of members to the board of trustees, pamphlets, brochures, documents or any other material containing association information which are prepared by the executive director or the board and are delivered to the employers for distribution.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.07.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/14">2950-4</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1931/0/406/#laws.0.4.0">1931 c 406 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/708/#laws.0.1.0">1949 c 708 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/277/#laws.1.76.0">2008 c 277 art 1 s 76</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.60.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.7.2.0">2010 c 359 art 7 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.8.4.0">2013 c 111 art 8 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.29.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.8.23.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 8 s 23</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.071"><b>354.071</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.6.3.0">2007 c 134 art 6 s 3</a>]</div>

      <div class="sr_by_subd" id="stat.354.09">
        <h1>354.09 </h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.09.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.09.1">§</a>

          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.276.0">1981 c 224 s 276</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.09.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.09.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.09.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.09.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.09.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.09.4">§</a>

          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.276.0">1981 c 224 s 276</a>]</p>

          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.09.5">§</a>
