      <div class="section" id="stat.354.091">
        <h1 class="shn">354.091 SERVICE CREDIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.091.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.091.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition; monthly base salary.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, "monthly base salary" means the annual base salary, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05">354.05</a>, subdivision 41, divided by 12.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.091.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.091.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Service credit annual limit.</span></h2>
          <p> No teacher may receive credit for more than one year of teaching service for any fiscal year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.091.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.091.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Service credit calculation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as specified in subdivisions 4 and 5, service credit must be calculated monthly by dividing the teacher's monthly salary by the monthly base salary for the teacher's employing unit and multiplying the result by 11.1 percent.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of computing service credit, salary must be allocated to each calendar month based on the pay period begin and end dates. If the pay period covers more than one calendar month, the salary must be allocated based on the number of days in each calendar month.</p>
          <p>(c) A teacher may not receive more than 11.1 percent of a year's service credit in a calendar month.</p>
          <p>(d) Annual service credit must be calculated by adding the allowable monthly service credit for all 12 months of the fiscal year, with the result rounded to two decimal places, subject to the annual limit specified in subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.091.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.091.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Service credit determination for Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system teachers.</span></h2>
          <p> For all services rendered on or after July 1, 2003, service credit for all members employed by the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system must be determined:</p>
          <p>(1) for full-time employees, by the definition of full-time employment contained in the collective bargaining agreement for those units listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/179A.10#stat.179A.10.2">179A.10, subdivision 2</a>, or contained in the applicable personnel or salary plan for those positions designated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/179A.10#stat.179A.10.1">179A.10, subdivision 1</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) for part-time employees, by the appropriate proration of full-time equivalency based on the provisions contained in the collective bargaining agreement for those units listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/179A.10#stat.179A.10.2">179A.10, subdivision 2</a>, or contained in the applicable personnel or salary plan for those positions designated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/179A.10#stat.179A.10.1">179A.10, subdivision 1</a>, and the applicable procedures of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.091.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.091.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Service credit procedure, nontraditional schedules.</span></h2>
          <p>For employer units that have nontraditional work schedules or pay schedules, the procedure for determining service credit must be specified by the executive director with the approval of the board of trustees.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.091.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.13.0">1974 c 289 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/160/#laws.0.3.0">1981 c 160 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/578/#laws.1.6.0">1982 c 578 art 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.2.8.0">1989 c 319 art 2 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.3.11.0">1994 c 528 art 3 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.29.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.3.4.0">2004 c 267 art 3 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.52.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.7.3.0">2010 c 359 art 7 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.092">
        <h1 class="shn">354.092 SABBATICAL LEAVE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.092.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.092.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>A sabbatical leave for the purpose of this section means a sabbatical leave as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.49">122A.49</a> or the applicable personnel policy of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.092.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.092.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Pay rate; certification.</span></h2>
          <p>A sabbatical leave must be compensated by a minimum of one-third of the salary that the member received for a comparable period during the prior fiscal year. Upon granting a sabbatical leave, the employing unit granting the leave must certify the leave to the association on a form specified by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.092.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.092.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Employer and employee contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>Employer contributions and deductions for employee contributions at the applicable rate specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.42">354.42</a> must be made by the employing unit based on the full normal base contract salary that would have been paid to the member if the member were not on sabbatical leave. The employer must meet the reporting and remittance requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.52">354.52</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.092.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.092.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Service credit.</span></h2>
          <p>A member may not receive more than three years of allowable service credit in any ten consecutive years under this section. Notwithstanding the provisions of any agreements to the contrary, employee and employer contributions may not be made to receive allowable service credit under this section if the member does not retain the right to full reinstatement both during and at the end of the sabbatical leave.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.092.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.54.0">1974 c 289 s 54</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/306/#laws.0.8.0">1975 c 306 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/160/#laws.0.4.0">1981 c 160 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/12/#laws.11.4.0">1Sp1985 c 12 art 11 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.2.9.0">1989 c 319 art 2 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.10.0">1991 c 340 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/241/#laws.3.4.0">1997 c 241 art 3 s 4</a>-6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.11.3.0">1998 c 397 art 11 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.30.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.30.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 30</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.093">
        <h1 class="shn">354.093 PARENTAL LEAVE.</h1>
        <p class="in">Upon granting a parental leave for the birth or adoption of a child, the employing unit granting the leave must certify the leave to the association on a form specified by the executive director. A member of the association granted parental leave of absence by the employing unit is entitled to service credit not to exceed one year for the period of leave upon payment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.72">354.72</a>. Notwithstanding the provisions of any agreements to the contrary, the contributions specified in this section may not be made to receive allowable service credit under this section if the member does not retain the right to full reinstatement at the end of the leave.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.093.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/329/#laws.0.29.0">1976 c 329 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.11.0">1991 c 340 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/141/#laws.3.20.0">1995 c 141 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.31.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.32.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 32</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.094">
        <h1 class="shn">354.094 EXTENDED LEAVES OF ABSENCE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.094.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.094.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Service credit contributions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Upon granting any extended leave of absence under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.46">122A.46</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136F.43">136F.43</a>, the employing unit granting the leave must certify the leave to the association on a form specified by the executive director. A member granted an extended leave of absence under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.46">122A.46</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136F.43">136F.43</a> shall receive allowable and formula service credit toward annuities and other benefits under this chapter, for each year of the leave, provided that payment is made as specified in paragraph (b). The employer may enter into an agreement with the exclusive bargaining representative of the teachers in the district under which, for an individual teacher, all or a portion of the employee's contribution is paid by the employer. Any such agreement must include a sunset of eligibility to qualify for the payment and must not be a part of the collective bargaining agreement. The leave period must not exceed five years. A member may not receive more than five years of allowable or formula service credit under this section.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Employee contribution payments without interest for the years for which a member is receiving service credit while on extended leave must be made on or before June 30 of each fiscal year for which service credit is to be received, or in instances of late reporting by the employer, within 30 days after the association gives notification to the member of the amount due. Payment is authorized after June 30 as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.72">354.72</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Notwithstanding the provisions of any agreements to the contrary, payments may not be made to receive allowable and formula service credit if the member does not have full reinstatement rights as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.46">122A.46</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136F.43">136F.43</a>, both during and at the end of the extended leave.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Any school district paying the employee's retirement contributions or payments under this section shall forward to the Teachers Retirement Association a copy of the agreement executed by the school district and the employee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.094.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.094.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/130/#laws.0.38.0">1991 c 130 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.34.0">1991 c 340 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.094.1b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.094.1b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/130/#laws.0.38.0">1991 c 130 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.34.0">1991 c 340 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.094.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.094.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Membership; retention.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.49#stat.354.49.4">354.49, subdivision 4</a>, clause (3), a member on extended leave under subdivision 1 shall retain membership in the association if payment under subdivision 1, paragraph (b), or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.72">354.72</a>, is made, under the same terms and conditions as if the member had continued to teach in the district or the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.094.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.094.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Effect of nonpayment.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A member on extended leave of absence under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.46">122A.46</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136F.43">136F.43</a> who fails to make payments under subdivision 1, paragraph (b), or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.72">354.72</a>, for any given year of the leave shall be deemed to cease to render teaching services beginning in that year for purposes of this chapter and may not make payment to the fund for any subsequent year of the leave until full payment is made for all prior years of the leave. Failure to make payment does not affect the rights or obligations of the member or the member's employer under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.46">122A.46</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136F.43">136F.43</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.094.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.094.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Member who does not resume teaching.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A member who makes the payments specified in subdivision 1, paragraph (b), or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.72">354.72</a>, to the fund for the agreed maximum duration of an extended leave and who does not resume teaching in the first school year after that maximum duration has elapsed is deemed to cease to render teaching services beginning in that year for purposes of this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.094.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.094.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Discharge; layoff.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The provisions of this section do not apply to a member who is discharged or placed on unrequested leave of absence or retrenchment or layoff or whose contract is terminated while the member is on an extended leave of absence under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.46">122A.46</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136F.43">136F.43</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.094.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.094.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Limits on other service credit.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A member who makes the payments required under subdivision 1, paragraph (b), or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.72">354.72</a>, and receives allowable and formula service credit in the association under this section may not pay employee contributions or receive allowable or formula service credit for the same fiscal year in any other Minnesota public employee pension plan, except a firefighters relief association governed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.091">424A.091</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.096">424A.096</a>. This subdivision must not be construed to prohibit a member who receives allowable and formula service credit in the association under this section for a given year from being employed as a substitute teacher by any school district during that year. Notwithstanding the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.091">354.091</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.42">354.42</a>, a teacher may not pay retirement contributions or receive allowable or formula service credit in the association for teaching service rendered for any part of any year for which the teacher pays retirement contributions or receives allowable or formula service credit under this section or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A.091">354A.091</a> while on an extended leave of absence under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.46">122A.46</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.094.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/447/#laws.9.3.0">1977 c 447 art 9 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/764/#laws.0.116.0">1978 c 764 s 116</a>,117; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/334/#laws.8.14.0">1979 c 334 art 8 s 14</a>,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/454/#laws.0.2.0">1980 c 454 s 2</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/618/#laws.0.4.0">1980 c 618 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/358/#laws.8.9.0">1981 c 358 art 8 s 9</a>-12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/314/#laws.10.6.0">1983 c 314 art 10 s 6</a>-8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/12/#laws.11.5.0">1Sp1985 c 12 art 11 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/284/#laws.6.5.0">1987 c 284 art 6 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/293/#laws.0.77.0">1989 c 293 s 77</a>,78; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.9.20.0">1989 c 329 art 9 s 20</a>,21; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/130/#laws.0.34.0">1991 c 130 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.12.0">1991 c 340 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/598/#laws.3.1.0">1992 c 598 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/141/#laws.3.20.0">1995 c 141 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/305/#laws.1.80.0">1996 c 305 art 1 s 80</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.11.3.0">1998 c 397 art 11 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.32.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/1/#laws.2.22.0">1Sp2001 c 1 art 2 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/12/#laws.3.2.0">1Sp2003 c 12 art 3 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.3.4.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 3 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.33.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.5.80.0">2013 c 111 art 5 s 80</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.2.31.0">2024 c 102 art 2 s 31</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.095">
        <h1 class="shn">354.095 MEDICAL LEAVE.</h1>
        <p class="in">(a) Upon granting a medical leave, an employing unit must certify the leave to the association on a form specified by the executive director. A member of the association who is on an authorized medical leave of absence is entitled to receive allowable service credit, not to exceed five years, for the period of leave, upon making the prescribed payment to the fund under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.72">354.72</a>. A member may not receive more than one year of allowable service credit during any fiscal year by making payment under this section. A member may not receive disability benefits under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.48">354.48</a> and receive allowable service credit under this section for the same period of time.</p>
        <p class="in">(b) The executive director shall reject an application for disability benefits under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.48">354.48</a> if the member is applying only because an employer-sponsored provider of private disability insurance benefits requires the application and the member would not have applied for disability benefits in the absence of the requirement. The member shall submit a copy of the disability insurance policy that requires an application for disability benefits from the plan if the member wishes to assert that the application is only being submitted because of the disability insurance policy requirement.</p>
        <p class="in">(c) Notwithstanding the provisions of any agreement to the contrary, employee and employer contributions may not be made to receive allowable service credit under this section if the member does not retain the right to full reinstatement both during and at the end of the medical leave.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.095.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.7.1.0">1990 c 570 art 7 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.13.0">1991 c 340 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/141/#laws.3.20.0">1995 c 141 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.34.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.11.5.0">2018 c 211 art 11 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.096">
        <h1 class="shn">354.096 FAMILY LEAVE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.096.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.096.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Certification.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon granting a family leave to a member, an employing unit must certify the leave to the association on a form specified by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.096.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.096.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Payment.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding any laws to the contrary, a member who is granted a family leave under United States Code, title 42, section 12631, may receive allowable service credit for the leave by making payment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.72">354.72</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.096.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.096.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Subsequent eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>The member shall return to public service after the leave period under United States Code, title 42, section 12631, to receive allowable service for a subsequent authorized family leave.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.096.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.3.12.0">1994 c 528 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.3.5.0">2004 c 267 art 3 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.35.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.28.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 28</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.10">
        <h1 class="shn">354.10 FUNDS NOT SUBJECT TO ASSIGNMENT OR PROCESS; BENEFICIARIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.10.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.10.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Exemption; exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.401">356.401</a> apply to the teachers retirement plan.</p>
          <p>(b) The right of a teacher to take advantage of the benefits provided by this chapter, is a personal right only and is not assignable. All money to the credit of a teacher's account in the fund or any money payable to the teacher from the fund belongs to the state of Minnesota until actually paid to the teacher or a beneficiary under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(c) The association may acknowledge a properly completed power of attorney form.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.10.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.10.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.81.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 81</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.10.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.10.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Payment to public bodies.</span></h2>
          <p>If, in the judgment of the executive director, conditions so warrant, payment of an annuity, a retirement benefit, or a refund may be made to a public body in behalf of an annuitant, disabilitant, or survivor upon such terms as the executive director may prescribe.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.10.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.10.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Changes in designated beneficiaries.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A beneficiary designated by a retiree or member under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.22">354.05, subdivision 22</a>, may be changed or revoked by the retiree or member on a form provided by the executive director.</p>
          <p>(b) A change or revocation made under this subdivision is valid only if the properly completed form is received by the association on or before the date of death of the retiree or the member.</p>
          <p>(c) If a designated beneficiary dies before the retiree or member designating the beneficiary and a new beneficiary is not designated, the retiree's or member's estate is the beneficiary.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.10.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/14">2950-10</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1931/0/406/#laws.0.10.0">1931 c 406 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/361/#laws.0.8.0">1955 c 361 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/745/#laws.0.1.0">1961 c 745 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/789/#laws.0.6.0">1971 c 789 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.14.0">1974 c 289 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/306/#laws.0.9.0">1975 c 306 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/67/#laws.0.5.0">1977 c 67 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/772/#laws.0.62.0">1978 c 772 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/303/#laws.3.30.0">1979 c 303 art 3 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/547/#laws.0.7.0">1984 c 547 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/157/#laws.0.6.0">1987 c 157 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.2.10.0">1989 c 319 art 2 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.14.0">1991 c 340 s 14</a>-16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.3.13.0">1994 c 528 art 3 s 13</a>,14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/141/#laws.3.20.0">1995 c 141 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/203/#laws.6.92.0">1997 c 203 art 6 s 92</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.9.6.0">1999 c 222 art 9 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.33.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.3.20.0">2003 c 127 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.53.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 53</a>-55</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.105">
        <h1 class="shn">354.105 PAYMENTS USING PRETAX TRANSFERS.</h1>
        <p class="in">If a current or past member is making a payment to the Teachers Retirement Association to receive service credit under a provision of this chapter, chapter 356, or applicable special law, and this payment is to be made by a transfer of pretax assets authorized under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.441">356.441</a>, payment is authorized after the due date, but not to exceed 90 days, provided that the authorization for the asset transfer has been received by the applicable third-party administrator by the due date, and the payment must include interest at the applicable monthly rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59">356.59, subdivision </a>4, compounded annually, from the due date through the end of the month in which the Teachers Retirement Association receives the payment.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.105.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.36.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.8.10.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 8 s 10</a></p>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.354.35">
        <h1 class="shn">354.35 OPTIONAL ACCELERATED RETIREMENT ANNUITY BEFORE NORMAL RETIREMENT AGE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.35.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.35.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Normal retirement age definition.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For purposes of this section, "normal retirement age" means normal retirement age as defined in United States Code, title 42, section 416(l), as amended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.35.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.35.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Election of accelerated annuity.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Any coordinated member who retires before normal retirement age may elect to receive an optional accelerated retirement annuity from the association which provides for different annuity amounts over different periods of retirement. The optional accelerated retirement annuity must take the form of an annuity payable for the period before the member attains age 65, or normal retirement age, in a greater amount than the amount of the annuity calculated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44">354.44</a> on the basis of the age of the member at retirement, but the optional accelerated retirement annuity must be the actuarial equivalent of the member's annuity computed on the basis of the member's age at retirement. The greater amount must be paid until the retiree reaches age 65, or normal retirement age, and at that time the payment from the association must be reduced. For each year the retiree is under age 65, or normal retirement age, up to five percent of the total life annuity required reserves may be used to accelerate the optional retirement annuity under this section.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Members who retire before age 62 may elect to have the annuity under this subdivision accelerated to age 62 rather than normal retirement age or age 65.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The method of computing the optional accelerated retirement annuity provided in this subdivision is established by the board of trustees. In establishing the method of computing the optional accelerated retirement annuity or any modification of that procedure, the board of trustees must obtain the written approval of the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>. The written approval must be a part of the permanent records of the board of trustees. The election of an optional accelerated retirement annuity is exercised by making an application on a form provided by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.35.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.35.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>An annuity under this section is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.35.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/15/#laws.0.5.0">Ex1957 c 15 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.18.0">1974 c 289 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/259/#laws.0.33.0">1987 c 259 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.2.11.0">1989 c 319 art 2 s 11</a>; art 13 s 54; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.18.0">1991 c 340 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.5.1.0">1993 c 307 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.34.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.3.47.0">2006 c 271 art 3 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.37.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.62.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.155.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 155</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.36"><b>354.36</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.37"><b>354.37</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.38"><b>354.38</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.39"><b>354.39</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.65.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 65</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.41">
        <h1 class="shn">354.41 TEACHERS RETIREMENT ASSOCIATION, ELIGIBILITY FOR MEMBERSHIP.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Teachers.</span></h2>
          <p>Every teacher in the service of the state or one of its governmental subdivisions as a teacher, except persons specifically excluded, must become a member of the association by the acceptance of such employment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.2.28.0">1989 c 319 art 2 s 28</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Membership eligibility for labor organization employees.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person who is a member on an authorized leave of absence and is employed as an employee or officer by a labor organization that is the exclusive bargaining agent or the labor organization's state affiliate representing teachers covered by this chapter or by an association of school administrators may elect to be a coordinated member of the association based on that employment, subject to the limitations set forth in subdivisions 4a and 4b. However, no person is entitled to membership under this section if the person also is a member of a teachers retirement association in a city of the first class organized under chapter 354A for the same period of service.</p>
          <p>(b) The election must be made within 90 days of commencing employment by the labor organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Limitations on salary and contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>The covered salary for a labor organization employee who qualifies for membership under this section is limited to the lesser of:</p>
          <p>(1) the employee's actual salary as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.35">354.05, subdivision 35</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) 75 percent of the salary of the governor as set under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15A.082">15A.082</a>.</p>
          <p>The limited covered salary determined under this subdivision must be used in determining employee, employer, and employer additional contributions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.42">354.42, subdivisions 2, 3, and 5</a>, and in determining retirement annuities and other benefits under this chapter and chapter 356.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.4b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.4b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Earning restrictions apply.</span></h2>
          <p>A retirement annuity is only payable, if the person has met any other applicable requirements, upon the termination by the person who elected coverage under subdivision 4 of employment by the labor organization. The reemployed annuitant earnings limitation set forth in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.5">354.44, subdivision 5</a>, applies in the event that the person who elected coverage under subdivision 4 retires and is subsequently reemployed while an annuitant by the labor organization or by any other entity employing persons who are covered by the Minnesota Teachers Retirement Association by virtue of that employment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Contributions for labor organization employees.</span></h2>
          <p>For all members described in subdivision 4, the employee, employer and additional employer contributions shall be the obligation of the employee who elects coverage herein; provided, however, that the employer organization enumerated in subdivision 4 employing such member may pay the employer and additional employer contributions. The employer shall, in any event, deduct the necessary contributions from the employee's salary and remit all contributions to the teachers retirement association pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.42">354.42</a>, except that if any such member is on leave of absence from a governmental employer unit, these contributions shall be forwarded by the employer organization to the governmental employer unit for remittance to the fund as described in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.276.0">1981 c 224 s 276</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>Members of the association as described in subdivision 4 or 9 and employees of the teachers retirement association are not eligible for election to the board of trustees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.276.0">1981 c 224 s 276</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.41.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.41.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/10/#laws.12.3.0">1Sp2001 c 10 art 12 s 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.41.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/16/#laws.0.3.0">Ex1957 c 16 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/50/#laws.0.27.0">Ex1959 c 50 s 27</a>,28; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/693/#laws.0.1.0">1967 c 693 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/40/#laws.0.15.0">1971 c 40 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/728/#laws.0.13.0">1973 c 728 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/306/#laws.0.10.0">1975 c 306 s 10</a>-13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/67/#laws.0.6.0">1977 c 67 s 6</a>,13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/97/#laws.0.2.0">1977 c 97 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/796/#laws.0.41.0">1978 c 796 s 41</a>,42; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/160/#laws.0.5.0">1981 c 160 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.13.55.0">1989 c 319 art 13 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.19.0">1991 c 340 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/598/#laws.3.2.0">1992 c 598 art 3 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/465/#laws.3.31.0">1994 c 465 art 3 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.4.8.0">1994 c 528 art 4 s 8</a>-10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/141/#laws.3.20.0">1995 c 141 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/10/#laws.12.1.0">1Sp2001 c 10 art 12 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.58.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 58</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.42">
        <h1 class="shn">354.42 CONTRIBUTIONS BY EMPLOYER AND EMPLOYEE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Teachers retirement fund.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Within the Teachers Retirement Association and the state treasury is created a special retirement fund, which must include all the assets of the Teachers Retirement Association and all revenue of the association.</p>
          <p>(b) The teachers retirement fund must be credited with all employee and employer contributions, all investment revenue and gains, and all other income authorized by law.</p>
          <p>(c) From the teachers retirement fund is appropriated the payments of annuities and benefits authorized by this chapter and the reasonable and necessary expenses of administering the fund and the association.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Employee contribution.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The employee contribution to the fund is the following percentage of the member's salary:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:3.571428571428571%">
            <col style="width:46.4285714285714%">
            <col style="width:25.1428571428571%">
            <col style="width:24.8571428571429%">
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">Period</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">Basic Program</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">Coordinated Program</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2014, through June 30, 2023</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">11 percent</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">7.5 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2023, through June 30, 2025</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">11.25 percent</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">7.75 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>after June 30, 2025</td>
              <td>11.5 percent</td>
              <td>8.0 percent</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(b) When an employee contribution rate changes for a fiscal year, the new contribution rate is effective for the entire salary paid for each employer unit with the first payroll cycle reported.</p>
          <p>(c) This contribution must be made by deduction from salary. Where any portion of a member's salary is paid from other than public funds, the member's employee contribution must be based on the entire salary received.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Employer.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The regular employer contribution to the fund by Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, is an amount equal to the applicable following percentage of salary of each coordinated member and the applicable percentage of salary of each basic member specified in paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>The additional employer contribution to the fund by Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, is an amount equal to 3.64 percent of the salary of each teacher who is a coordinated member or who is a basic member.</p>
          <p>(b) The regular employer contribution to the fund by Independent School District No. 709, Duluth, is an amount equal to the applicable percentage of salary of each old law or new law coordinated member specified for the coordinated program in paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>(c) The employer contribution to the fund for every other employer is an amount equal to the applicable following percentage of the salary of each coordinated member and the applicable following percentage of the salary of each basic member:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:3.840245775729647%">
            <col style="width:45.3149001536098%">
            <col style="width:5.069124423963133%">
            <col style="width:19.3548387096774%">
            <col style="width:3.993855606758833%">
            <col style="width:22.4270353302611%">
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">Period</td>
              <td colspan="2">Coordinated Member</td>
              <td></td>
              <td>Basic Member</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2022, through June 30, 2023</td>
              <td></td>
              <td>8.55 percent</td>
              <td></td>
              <td>12.55 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2023, through June 30, 2025</td>
              <td></td>
              <td>8.75 percent</td>
              <td></td>
              <td>12.75 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>after June 30, 2025</td>
              <td></td>
              <td>9.5 percent</td>
              <td></td>
              <td>13.5 percent</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(d) When an employer contribution rate changes for a fiscal year, the new contribution rate is effective for the entire salary paid for each employer unit with the first payroll cycle reported.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/485/#laws.0.40.0">1969 c 485 s 40</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.3.4.0">2018 c 211 art 3 s 4</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.4b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.4b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.</h2>
          <p>MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.3.4.0">2018 c 211 art 3 s 4</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.4c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.4c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4c.</h2>
          <p>MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.3.4.0">2018 c 211 art 3 s 4</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.4d">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4d." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.4d">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4d.</h2>
          <p>MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.3.4.0">2018 c 211 art 3 s 4</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/233/#laws.1.50.0">1997 c 233 art 1 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.42.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.42.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Erroneous salary deductions or direct payments.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any deductions taken from the salary of an employee for the retirement fund in excess of amounts required must be refunded to the employee upon the discovery of the error and after the verification of the error by the employing unit making the deduction. The corresponding excess employer contribution and excess additional employer contribution amounts attributable to the erroneous salary deduction must be refunded to the employing unit.</p>
          <p>(b) If salary deductions and employer contributions were erroneously transmitted to the retirement fund and should have been transmitted to the plan covered by chapter 352D, 353D, 354B, or 354D, the executive director must transfer these salary deductions and employer contributions to the account of the appropriate person under the applicable plan. The transfer to the applicable defined contribution plan account must include interest at the applicable monthly rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59">356.59</a>, subdivision 4, compounded annually, from the first day of the month following the month in which coverage should have commenced in the defined contribution plan until the end of the month in which the transfer occurs.</p>
          <p>(c) A potential transfer under paragraph (b) that would cause the plan to fail to be a qualified plan under section 401(a) of the Internal Revenue Code, as amended, must not be made by the executive director. Within 30 days after being notified by the Teachers Retirement Association of an unmade potential transfer under this paragraph, the employer of the affected person must transmit an amount representing the applicable salary deductions and employer contributions, without interest, to the account of the applicable person under the appropriate plan. The retirement association must provide a credit for the amount of the erroneous salary deductions and employer contributions against future contributions from the employer.</p>
          <p>(d) If a salary payment from which a deduction for the retirement fund was taken has been canceled or the amount of the payment has been returned to the funds of the employing unit making the payment, a refund of the amount deducted, or any portion of it that is required to adjust the salary deductions, must be made to the employing unit.</p>
          <p>(e) Erroneous direct payments of member-paid contributions or erroneous salary deductions that were not refunded during the regular payroll cycle processing must be refunded to the member, plus interest computed using the rate and method specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.49#stat.354.49.2">354.49, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(f) Any refund under this subdivision that would cause the plan to fail to be a qualified plan under section 401(a) of the Internal Revenue Code, as amended, may not be refunded and instead must be credited against future contributions payable by the employer. The employer is responsible for refunding to the applicable employee any amount that was erroneously deducted from the salary of the employee, with interest as specified in paragraph (e).</p>
          <p>(g) If erroneous employee deductions and employer contributions are caused by an error in plan coverage involving the plan and any other plan specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.637">356.637</a>, that section applies.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.42.history
			">

      <div class="sr_by_subd" id="stat.354.43">
