      <div class="section" id="stat.354.435">
        <h1 class="shn">354.435 ADDITIONAL CONTRIBUTIONS BY SPECIAL SCHOOL DISTRICT NO. 1 AND CITY OF MINNEAPOLIS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.435.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.435.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Special direct state matching aid.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, and the city of Minneapolis must make additional employer contributions to the Teachers Retirement Association in the amounts specified in paragraph (b). These contributions can be made from any available source. If made in whole or in part by a levy, the levy may be classified as that of a special taxing district for purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.065">275.065</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/276.04">276.04</a>, and for all other property tax purposes.</p>
          <p>(b) Each fiscal year $1,250,000 must be contributed by Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, and $1,250,000 must be contributed by the city of Minneapolis to the Teachers Retirement Association, and the state shall match this total by paying to the Teachers Retirement Association $2,500,000. The superintendent of Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, the mayor of the city of Minneapolis, and the executive director of the Teachers Retirement Association shall jointly certify to the commissioner of management and budget the total amount that has been contributed by Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, and by the city of Minneapolis to the Teachers Retirement Association. Any certification to the commissioner of management and budget must be made quarterly. If the certifications for a fiscal year exceed the maximum annual direct state matching aid amount in any quarter, the amount of direct state matching aid payable to the Teachers Retirement Association must be limited to the balance of the maximum annual direct state matching aid amount available. The amount required under this paragraph, subject to the maximum direct state matching aid amount, is appropriated annually to the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner of management and budget may prescribe the form of the certifications required under paragraph (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.435.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.435.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Additional contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>In addition to any other required contributions, on or before June 30 each fiscal year, Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, and the city of Minneapolis must each make an additional contribution to the Teachers Retirement Association of $1,000,000.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.435.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.435.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Procedure for recovery of deficient or delinquent amounts.</span></h2>
          <p>If Special School District No. 1, Minneapolis, or the city of Minneapolis fails to pay the full amount required under subdivision 1, paragraph (b), or 2, in a timely manner, the executive director is authorized to use section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.512">354.512</a>, or any other process in law to ensure full payment is obtained.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.435.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.435.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Aid expiration.</span></h2>
          <p>The aid amounts specified in this section must continue until the earlier of:</p>
          <p>(1) the first day of the fiscal year following three consecutive fiscal years in which, for each fiscal year, the actuarial value of assets of the fund equals or exceeds 100 percent of the actuarial accrued liabilities as reported by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a> in the annual actuarial valuation prepared under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215">356.215</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) July 1, 2048.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.435.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/286/#laws.8.3.0">2012 c 286 art 8 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.8.2.0">2018 c 211 art 8 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.9.2.0">2024 c 102 art 9 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.436">
        <h1 class="shn">354.436 DIRECT STATE AID ON BEHALF OF THE FORMER FIRST CLASS CITY TEACHERS RETIREMENT FUND ASSOCIATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.436.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.436.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Aid authorization.</span></h2>
          <p>The state shall pay $12,954,000 to the Teachers Retirement Association on behalf of the former Minneapolis Teachers Retirement Fund Association and shall pay $14,377,000 during fiscal year 2015 to the Teachers Retirement Association for the credit of the Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association and, after fiscal year 2015, shall pay $14,377,000 on behalf of the Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.436.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.436.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Aid appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of management and budget shall pay the aid amounts under subdivision 1 annually on October 1. The amount required is appropriated annually from the general fund to the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.436.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.436.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Aid expiration.</span></h2>
          <p>The aid amounts specified in this section continue until the earlier of:</p>
          <p>(1) the first day of the fiscal year following three consecutive fiscal years in which, for each fiscal year, the actuarial value of assets of the fund equals or exceeds 100 percent of the actuarial accrued liabilities as reported by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a> in the annual actuarial valuation prepared under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215">356.215</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) July 1, 2048.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.436.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.13.1.0">2013 c 111 art 13 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.8.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 8</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.8.3.0">2018 c 211 art 8 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.9.3.0">2024 c 102 art 9 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.44">
        <h1 class="shn">354.44 RETIREMENT BENEFITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements as to age and service.</span></h2>
          <p>Any member or former member who ceases or has ceased to render teaching services in any school or institution covered by the provisions of this chapter, and who has attained the age of at least 55 years with not less than three years allowable service, or who has received credit for not less than 30 years allowable service regardless of age, is entitled upon written application to a retirement annuity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Proportionate annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>A member who terminates employment at any time during the academic year at the end of which the person is at the normal retirement age or older shall, for the purpose of determining eligibility for a proportionate retirement annuity, be considered to have been required to terminate employment at normal retirement age or older pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.32">356.32</a>. For purposes of this subdivision, an academic year ends August 31.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Computation of money purchase annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The amount of retirement annuity is an amount equal to double the annuity which could be purchased by the member's accumulated deductions plus interest thereon. The annuity must be determined by the member's age, sex, double the amount of accumulated deductions, double the amount of interest earned on the accumulated deductions, and the appropriate mortality tables and interest rates. To determine the amount of the annuity for a basic member, the accumulated deductions before July 1, 1957, and the accumulated deductions after July 1, 1957, must be considered separately.</p>
          <p>(b) For service rendered before July 1, 1957, the accumulated deductions for a member must be carried forward at a fixed amount which is shown credited to the member's account as of that date. That fixed amount must also include any payments in lieu of salary deductions which were actually so made under an agreement executed between the member and the board as authorized by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.50">354.50</a> or any other authorized payments made by the member to the fund. The annuity granted with respect to the period must be determined as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) the fixed amount of the accumulated deductions for the period including the interest credited on the amount as earned up to July 1, 1957; and</p>
          <p>(2) annuity purchase rates based on the applicable mortality table established by the board and the interest rate assumption in effect before July 1, 1957, in the case of basic members and an annuity purchase rate based on an appropriate annuity table of mortality established by the board as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.07#stat.354.07.1">354.07, subdivision 1</a>, and using the applicable investment return assumption specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>, in the case of coordinated members.</p>
          <p>(c) For service rendered after July 1, 1957, the accumulated deductions for a member must consist of the amounts actually credited to the member's account by reason of salary deductions. The annuity granted with respect to the period must be determined by the following:</p>
          <p>(1) accumulated deductions for the period;</p>
          <p>(2) interest credited on these accumulated deductions from July 1, 1957, to the date of retirement;</p>
          <p>(3) interest credited on accumulated deductions including prior credited interest provided in paragraph (b) from July 1, 1957, to the date of retirement;</p>
          <p>(4) after the amount available for an annuity granted with respect to the person is determined in accordance with the provisions of this subdivision, an additional amount equal to 20 percent of the sum of clause (1) plus interest credited to a member's account from July 1, 1957, to date of retirement is to be added. This added amount is not to be doubled as provided for other amounts determined in this subdivision; and</p>
          <p>(5) the annuity purchase rate based on an appropriate annuity table of mortality established by the board as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.07#stat.354.07.1">354.07, subdivision 1</a>, and using the applicable investment return assumption specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Application for retirement.</span></h2>
          <p>A member or a person authorized to act on behalf of the member may make application for retirement provided the age and service requirements under subdivision 1 are satisfied on or before the member's retirement annuity accrual date under subdivision 4. The application may be made no earlier than 180 days before the termination of teaching service. The application must be made on a form prescribed by the executive director and is not complete until all necessary supporting documents are received by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Retirement annuity accrual date.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the applicable age and service requirements under this section are satisfied, an annuity payment begins to accrue as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) on the day after the termination of teaching service;</p>
          <p>(2) on the day of receipt of application if the application is filed with the executive director after the six-month period that occurs immediately following the termination of teaching service; or</p>
          <p>(3) on July 1 for all school principals and other administrators who receive a full annual contract salary during the fiscal year for performance of a full year's contract duties.</p>
          <p>(b) A member, or a person authorized to act on behalf of the member, may specify a different date of retirement from that determined in paragraph (a), as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) if the application is filed on or before the date of termination of teaching service, the accrual date must not be earlier than the day after the termination of teaching service and no later than six months after the termination date; or</p>
          <p>(2) if the application is filed during the six-month period that occurs immediately following the termination of teaching service, the annuity may begin to accrue retroactively, but no earlier than the day after termination of teaching service and no later than six months after the termination date.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Resumption of teaching service after retirement.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Any person who retired under the provisions of this chapter and has thereafter resumed teaching in any employer unit to which this chapter applies is eligible to continue to receive payments in accordance with the annuity except that all or a portion of the annuity payments must be deferred during the calendar year immediately following the fiscal year in which the person's salary from the teaching service is in an amount greater than $46,000. The amount of the annuity deferral is one-half of the salary amount in excess of $46,000 and must be deducted from the annuity payable for the calendar year immediately following the fiscal year in which the excess amount was earned.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the person is retired for only a fractional part of the fiscal year during the initial year of retirement, the maximum reemployment salary exempt from triggering a deferral as specified in this subdivision must be prorated for that fiscal year.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) After a person has reached the Social Security normal retirement age, no deferral requirement is applicable regardless of the amount of salary.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The amount of the retirement annuity deferral must be handled or disposed of as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.47">356.47</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) For the purpose of this subdivision, salary from teaching service includes all salary or income earned as a teacher as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.2">354.05, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), clause (1). Salary from teaching service also includes, but is not limited to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) all income for services performed as a consultant, independent contractor, or third-party supplier, or as an employee of a consultant, independent contractor, or third-party supplier, to an employer unit covered by the provisions of this chapter; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the greater of either the income received or an amount based on the rate paid with respect to an administrative position, consultant, independent contractor, or third-party supplier, or as an employee of a consultant, independent contractor, or third-party supplier, in an employer unit with approximately the same number of pupils and at the same level as the position occupied by the person who resumes teaching service.</p>
          <p>(f) Notwithstanding other paragraphs of this subdivision, if the reemployed annuitant has a former spouse receiving a portion of the annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.58#stat.518.58.1">518.58, subdivision 1</a>, the portion payable to the former spouse must not be deferred.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.5a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.5a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Exemption for interim superintendent.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who performs services as an interim superintendent because of the death, disability, termination, or resignation of the previous superintendent is exempt from the earnings limitations and reductions in annuity payments in subdivision 5 for up to 90 working days of service as an interim superintendent. During this period of up to 90 working days, the school board may pay the interim superintendent at any rate, up to the rate paid to the previous superintendent. This exemption applies only if the school board hiring the interim superintendent submits an application for the exemption on a form prescribed by the executive director, and the executive director approves the application before the services as interim superintendent begin. The application must certify that the school board has unanimously approved the exemption from the earnings limitations and reductions. The executive director may prescribe a form for the application. A school board shall not apply for more than one exemption in a fiscal year. No more than three exemptions may be approved for any person. Only one exemption may be approved for any person in a fiscal year. The exemption under this subdivision does not apply to a person who retires from a school district and within one year after retirement returns to the same school district as an interim superintendent.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Computation of formula program retirement annuity.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The formula retirement annuity must be computed in accordance with the applicable provisions of the formulas stated in paragraph (b) or (d) on the basis of each member's average salary under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.13a">354.05, subdivision 13a</a>, for the period of the member's formula service credit.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) This paragraph, in conjunction with paragraph (c), applies to a person who first became a member of the association or a member of a pension fund listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, before July 1, 1989, unless paragraph (d), in conjunction with paragraph (e), produces a higher annuity amount, in which case paragraph (d) applies. The average salary as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.13a">354.05, subdivision 13a</a>, multiplied by the following percentages per year of formula service credit shall determine the amount of the annuity to which the member qualifying therefor is entitled for service rendered before July 1, 2006:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:3.333333333333333%">
            <col style="width:29.1666666666667%">
            <col style="width:2.5%">
            <col style="width:33.3333333333333%">
            <col style="width:2.5%">
            <col style="width:29.1666666666667%">
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">Period</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">Coordinated Member</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">Basic Member</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">Each year of service during first ten </td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">1.2 percent  per year </td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">2.2 percent  per year</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">Each year of service thereafter </td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">1.7 percent  per year </td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">2.7 percent  per year</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p class="in">For service rendered on or after July 1, 2006, by a member other than a member who was a member of the former Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association between January 1, 2006, and June 30, 2015, and for service rendered on or after July 1, 2013, by a member who was a member of the former Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association between January 1, 2013, and June 30, 2015, the average salary as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.13a">354.05, subdivision 13a</a>, multiplied by the following percentages per year of service credit, determines the amount the annuity to which the member qualifying therefor is entitled:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:3.333333333333333%">
            <col style="width:29.1666666666667%">
            <col style="width:2.5%">
            <col style="width:33.3333333333333%">
            <col style="width:2.5%">
            <col style="width:29.1666666666667%">
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">Period</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">Coordinated Member</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">Basic Member</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">Each year of service during first ten </td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">1.4 percent  per year </td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">2.2 percent  per year</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">Each year of service after ten years of service </td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">1.9 percent  per year </td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;"></td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: left;">2.7 percent  per year</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p class="in">(c)(1) This paragraph applies only to a person who first became a member of the association or a member of a pension fund listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, before July 1, 1989, and whose annuity is higher when calculated under paragraph (b), in conjunction with this paragraph than when calculated under paragraph (d), in conjunction with paragraph (e).</p>
          <p class="in">(2) Where any member retires prior to normal retirement age under a formula annuity, the member shall be paid a retirement annuity in an amount equal to the normal annuity provided in paragraph (b) reduced by one-quarter of one percent for each month that the member is under normal retirement age at the time of retirement except that for any member who has 30 or more years of allowable service credit, the reduction shall be applied only for each month that the member is under age 62.</p>
          <p class="in">(3) Any member whose attained age plus credited allowable service totals 90 years is entitled, upon application, to a retirement annuity in an amount equal to the normal annuity provided in paragraph (b), without any reduction by reason of early retirement.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) This paragraph applies to a member who has become at least 55 years old and first became a member of the association after June 30, 1989, and to any other member who has become at least 55 years old and whose annuity amount when calculated under this paragraph and in conjunction with paragraph (e), is higher than it is when calculated under paragraph (b), in conjunction with paragraph (c).</p>
          <p class="in">(1) For a basic member, the average salary, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.13a">354.05, subdivision 13a</a>, multiplied by 2.7 percent for each year of service for a basic member determines the amount of the retirement annuity to which the basic member is entitled. The annuity of a basic member who was a member of the former Minneapolis Teachers Retirement Fund Association as of June 30, 2006, must be determined according to the annuity formula under the articles of incorporation of the former Minneapolis Teachers Retirement Fund Association in effect as of that date.</p>
          <p class="in">(2) For a coordinated member, the average salary, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.13a">354.05, subdivision 13a</a>, multiplied by 1.7 percent for each year of service rendered before July 1, 2006, and by 1.9 percent for each year of service rendered on or after July 1, 2006, for a member other than a member who was a member of the former Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association between January 1, 2006, and June 30, 2015, and by 1.9 percent for each year of service rendered on or after July 1, 2013, for a member of the former Duluth Teachers Retirement Fund Association between January 1, 2013, and June 30, 2015, determines the amount of the retirement annuity to which the coordinated member is entitled.</p>
          <p>(e) This paragraph applies to a person who has become at least 55 years old and first becomes a member of the association after June 30, 1989, and to any other member who has become at least 55 years old and whose annuity is higher when calculated under paragraph (d) in conjunction with this paragraph than when calculated under paragraph (b) in conjunction with paragraph (c). An employee who retires under the formula annuity before the normal retirement age is entitled to receive the normal annuity provided in paragraph (d), reduced as described in clause (1) or (2), as applicable.</p>
          <p>(1) For a member who is at least age 62 and has at least 30 years of service, the annuity shall be reduced by an early reduction factor of six percent for each year that the member's age of retirement precedes the normal retirement age. The resulting reduced annuity shall be further adjusted to take into account the increase in the monthly amount that would have occurred had the member retired early and deferred receipt of the annuity until normal retirement age and the annuity was augmented during the deferral period at 2.5 percent, if the member commenced employment after June 30, 2006, or at three percent, if the member commenced employment before July 1, 2006, compounded annually.</p>
          <p>(2) For a member who has not attained age 62 or has fewer than 30 years of service, the annuity shall be reduced for each year that the member's age of retirement precedes normal retirement age by the following early reduction factors:</p>
          <p>(i) for the period during which the member is age 55 through age 58, the factor is four percent; and</p>
          <p>(ii) for the period during which the member is at least age 59 but not yet normal retirement age, the factor is seven percent.</p>
          <p>The resulting annuity shall be further adjusted to take into account the increase in the monthly amount that would have occurred had the member retired early and deferred receipt of the annuity until normal retirement age and the annuity was augmented during the deferral period at the applicable annual rate, compounded annually. The applicable annual rate is the rate in effect for the month that includes the member's effective date of retirement and shall be considered as fixed for the member for the period until the member reaches normal retirement age. The applicable annual rate for June 2019 is 2.5 percent, if the member commenced employment after June 30, 2006, or three percent, if the member commenced employment before July 1, 2006, compounded annually, and decreases each month beginning July 2019 in equal monthly increments over the five-year period that begins July 1, 2019, and ends June 30, 2024, to zero percent effective for July 2024 and thereafter.</p>
          <p>After June 30, 2024, the reduced annuity commencing before normal retirement age under this clause shall not take into account any augmentation.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) No retirement annuity is payable to a former employee with a salary that exceeds 95 percent of the governor's salary unless and until the salary figures used in computing the highest five successive years average salary under paragraph (a) have been audited by the Teachers Retirement Association and determined by the executive director to comply with the requirements and limitations of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05">354.05</a>, subdivisions 35 and 35a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.6a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.6a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.37.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 37</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.64.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 64</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.7a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.7a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A retirement annuity under subdivision 2 or 6 is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Retirement annuities payable from the teachers retirement plan must not be in an amount less than the amount originally determined on the date of retirement and as adjusted on each succeeding January 1 under Minnesota Statutes 2008, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/11A.18">11A.18</a>, before January 1, 2010, and under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a> after December 31, 2009.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Annuity payment; provision of evidence of receipt.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An annuity or benefit for a given month must be paid during the first week of that month.</p>
          <p>(b) Evidence of receipt of the check issued or acknowledgment of the amount electronically transferred in payment of an annuity or benefit may be required from the payee on a form prescribed by the executive director. The evidence of receipt form may be required periodically at times specified by the board. If the filing of an evidence of receipt form is required and the form is not filed, future annuities or benefits must be withheld until the form is submitted.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.44.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.44.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Determining applicable law.</span></h2>
          <p>An annuity under this chapter must be computed under the law in effect as of the date of termination of teaching service. A former teacher who returns to covered service following a termination and who is not receiving a retirement annuity under this section must have earned at least one-half year of credited service following the return to covered service to be eligible for improved benefits resulting from any law change enacted subsequent to that termination.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.354.444">
        <h1 class="shn">354.444 RETURN TO WORK AGREEMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.444.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.444.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Authorization.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding any other provisions in this chapter, an eligible person as specified in subdivision 2 is authorized to commence receipt of a retirement annuity from the association and enter into an agreement to return to work. This provision must be administered in accordance with the federal Internal Revenue Code and applicable rulings.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.444.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.444.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">An eligible person is a person who:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) is a teacher as defined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.2">354.05, subdivision 2</a>, who is at least age 62;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) enters into a written agreement with the employing unit to return to work; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) retires under the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44">354.44</a> and begins to draw an annuity from the Teachers Retirement Association.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.444.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.444.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Work agreement.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Participation, the amount of time worked, and the duration of participation under this section must be mutually agreed upon by the employing unit and the employee. The employing unit may require up to a one-year notice of intent to participate in the program as a condition of participation. The employing unit shall determine the time of year the employee shall work. Unless otherwise specified in this section, the employing unit may not require a person to waive any rights under a collective bargaining agreement as a condition of participation under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.444.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.444.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Exclusion.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For purposes of this section, "employing unit" does not include the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.444.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.444.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">No service credit or contribution.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, an eligible person under this section may not, based on employment to which this section applies, contribute to or earn further service credit in the Teachers Retirement Association.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.444.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.444.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Annuity application procedure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A participant in the program specified in this section must apply for a retirement annuity under the application procedure specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44">354.44, subdivisions 3</a> and 4. A copy of the written agreement with the employing unit must be included with the person's retirement annuity application. This written agreement must include the termination date and reemployment date. The filing of the initial executed agreement must occur before reemployment under the agreement commences. The reemployment date must be after the member's accrual date.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.444.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.444.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Annuity treatment.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For purposes of the annuity deferral under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.5">354.44, subdivision 5</a>, an eligible person under this section is a reemployed annuitant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.444.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.444.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Continuing rights.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A person who returns to work under this section is a member of the appropriate bargaining unit and is covered by the appropriate collective bargaining contract. Except as provided in this section, the person's coverage is subject to any part of the contract limiting rights of part-time employees.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.444.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.3.9.0">2008 c 349 art 3 s 9</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.445">
        <h1 class="shn">354.445 NO ANNUITY REDUCTION.</h1>
        <p>(a) The annuity reduction provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.5">354.44, subdivision 5</a>, do not apply to a person who:</p>
        <p>(1) retires from the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system with at least ten years of combined service credit in a system under the jurisdiction of the Board of Trustees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities;</p>
        <p>(2) was employed on a full-time basis immediately preceding retirement as a faculty member or as an unclassified administrator in that system;</p>
        <p>(3) was not a recipient of an early retirement incentive under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136F.481">136F.481</a>;</p>
        <p>(4) begins drawing an annuity from the Teachers Retirement Association; and</p>
        <p>(5) returns to work on not less than a one-third time basis and not more than a two-thirds time basis in the system from which the person retired under an agreement in which the person may not earn a salary of more than $62,000 in a fiscal year through employment after retirement in the system from which the person retired.</p>
        <p>(b) Initial participation, the amount of time worked, and the duration of participation under this section must be mutually agreed upon by the president of the institution where the person returns to work and the employee. The president may require up to one-year notice of intent to participate in the program as a condition of participation under this section. The president shall determine the time of year the employee shall work. The employer or the president may not require a person to waive any rights under a collective bargaining agreement as a condition of participation under this section.</p>
        <p>(c) Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, a person eligible under paragraphs (a) and (b) may not, based on employment to which the waiver in this section applies, earn further service credit in a Minnesota public defined benefit plan and is not eligible to participate in a Minnesota public defined contribution plan, other than a volunteer fire plan governed by chapter 424A. No employer or employee contribution to any of these plans may be made on behalf of such a person.</p>
        <p>(d) For a person eligible under paragraphs (a) and (b) who earns more than $62,000 in a fiscal year through employment after retirement due to employment by the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system, the annuity reduction provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.5">354.44, subdivision 5</a>, apply only to income over $62,000.</p>
        <p>(e) A person who returns to work under this section is a member of the appropriate bargaining unit and is covered by the appropriate collective bargaining contract. Except as provided in this section, the person's coverage is subject to any part of the contract limiting rights of part-time employees.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.445.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/602/#laws.0.2.0">1994 c 602 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/212/#laws.4.64.0">1995 c 212 art 4 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/262/#laws.1.4.0">1995 c 262 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/390/#laws.2.9.0">1998 c 390 art 2 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.19.4.0">1999 c 222 art 19 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.2.7.0">2000 c 461 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.8.4.0">2014 c 296 art 8 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.12.35.0">2015 c 68 art 12 s 35</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.45">
        <h1 class="shn">354.45 OPTIONAL RETIREMENT ANNUITIES; UNPAID ANNUITIES, DISPOSITION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.45.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.45.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Optional annuity forms.</span></h2>
          <p>The retirement board shall establish optional annuities at retirement which shall take the form of an annuity payable for a period certain and for life thereafter or the form of a joint and survivor annuity. The board shall also establish an optional annuity which shall take the form of a guaranteed refund annuity paying the annuitant a fixed amount for life with the guarantee that in the event of death the balance of the accumulated deductions and interest accrued to the date of retirement will be paid to the designated beneficiary. Except as provided in subdivision 1a, any optional annuity forms shall be actuarially equivalent to the normal forms provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44">354.44</a>. In establishing these optional annuity forms, the board shall obtain the written recommendation of the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>. The recommendations shall be a part of the permanent records of the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.45.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.45.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Bounce-back annuity.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If a former member or disabilitant selects a joint and survivor annuity option under subdivision 1, the former member or disabilitant must receive a normal single life annuity if the designated optional annuity beneficiary dies before the former member or disabilitant. Under this option, no reduction may be made in the person's annuity to provide for restoration of the normal single life annuity in the event of the death of the designated optional annuity beneficiary.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The restoration of the normal single life annuity under this subdivision will take effect on the first of the month following the date of death of the designated optional annuity beneficiary, or on the first of the month following one year before the date on which a certified copy of the death record of the designated optional annuity beneficiary is received in the office of the Teachers Retirement Association, whichever date is later.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Except as stated in paragraph (b), this subdivision may not be interpreted as authorizing retroactive benefit payments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.45.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.45.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Refund upon death of retired member.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon the death of the member after retirement where no designated beneficiary shall survive the member, any remaining unpaid guaranteed annuity payments shall be commuted at the applicable investment return specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>, and paid in one sum to the estate of the member. Upon the death of the last designated beneficiary after the death of the member, the value of any unpaid guaranteed annuity payments shall be commuted at the applicable investment return specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>, and paid in one sum to the estate of the designated beneficiary.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.45.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.45.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Payment upon death of former spouse.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon the death of the former spouse to whom payments are to be made before the end of the specified payment period, payments shall be made according to the terms of a beneficiary form completed by the former spouse or, if no beneficiary form, to the estate of the former spouse or as otherwise ordered by a court of competent jurisdiction.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.45.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/16/#laws.0.7.0">Ex1957 c 16 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/1/17/#laws.0.12.0">Ex1961 c 17 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/845/#laws.0.5.0">1963 c 845 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/40/#laws.0.17.0">1971 c 40 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/728/#laws.0.20.0">1973 c 728 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/259/#laws.0.36.0">1987 c 259 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.13.60.0">1989 c 319 art 13 s 60</a>,61; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.32.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/9/#laws.15.32.0">1Sp2001 c 9 art 15 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.52.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.3.37.0">2006 c 271 art 3 s 37</a>,47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.38.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.34.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.11.8.0">2018 c 211 art 11 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.8.23.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 8 s 23</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.46">
        <h1 class="shn">354.46 PAYMENTS AFTER DEATH.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.46.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.46.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Basic program; benefits for spouse and children of teacher.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a basic member who has at least 18 months of allowable service credit and who has an average salary as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, equal to or greater than $75 dies prior to retirement or if a former basic member who, at the time of death, was totally and permanently disabled and receiving disability benefits pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.48">354.48</a> dies before attaining age 65 or reaching the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later, the surviving spouse and dependent children of the basic member or former basic member are entitled to receive a monthly benefit as follows:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:3.846153846153846%">
            <col style="width:34.6153846153846%">
            <col style="width:61.5384615384615%">
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td class="valign_top">
                <span>(1) Surviving spouse </span>
                <span class="leader">.</span>
              </td>
              <td>50 percent of the basic member's monthly average salary paid in the last full fiscal year preceding death</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td class="valign_top">
                <span>(2) Each dependent child </span>
                <span class="leader">.</span>
              </td>
              <td>ten percent of the basic member's monthly average salary paid in the last full fiscal year preceding death</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(b) Payments for the benefit of any dependent child under the age of 22 years must be made to the surviving parent, or if there be none, to the legal guardian of the child. The maximum monthly benefit must not exceed $1,000 for any one family, and the minimum benefit per family must not be less than 50 percent of the basic member's average salary, subject to the foregoing maximum. The surviving dependent children's benefit must be reduced pro tanto when any surviving child is no longer dependent.</p>
          <p>(c) If the basic member and the surviving spouse are killed in a common disaster and if the total of all survivors benefits payable pursuant to this subdivision is less than the accumulated deductions plus interest payable, the surviving dependent children shall receive the difference in a lump-sum payment.</p>
          <p>(d) If the survivor benefits provided in this subdivision exceed in total the monthly average salary of the deceased basic member, these benefits must be reduced to an amount equal to the deceased basic member's monthly average salary.</p>
          <p>(e) Prior to payment of any survivor benefit pursuant to this subdivision, in lieu of that benefit, the surviving spouse may elect to receive the joint and survivor annuity provided pursuant to subdivision 2, or may elect to receive a refund of accumulated deductions with interest in a lump sum as provided pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.47#stat.354.47.1">354.47, subdivision 1</a>. If there are any surviving dependent children, the surviving spouse may elect to receive the refund of accumulated deductions only with the consent of the district court of the district in which the surviving dependent child or children reside.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.46.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.46.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Surviving spouse survivor coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the active or deferred member was at least age 55 and had credit for at least three years of allowable service on the date of death, the surviving spouse is entitled to the second portion of a 100 percent joint and survivor annuity specified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.45">354.45</a>, based on the age of the active or deferred member at the time of death and the age of the surviving spouse at the time the benefit accrues.</p>
          <p>(b) If the active or deferred member was under age 55 and had credit for at least 30 years of allowable service on the date of death, the surviving spouse may elect to receive the second portion of a 100 percent joint and survivor annuity based on the age of the active or deferred member on the date of death and the age of the surviving spouse at the time the benefit accrues. If section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, applies, the annuity is payable using the full early retirement reduction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, paragraph (c), clause (2), to age 55 and one-half of the early retirement reduction from age 55 to the age payment begins.</p>
          <p>(c) If the active or deferred member was under age 55 and had credit for at least three years of allowable service on the date of death, but did not yet qualify for retirement, the surviving spouse may elect to receive the second portion of a 100 percent joint and survivor annuity based on the age of the active or deferred member at the time of death and the age of the surviving spouse at the time the benefit accrues. If section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, applies, the annuity is calculated using the full early retirement reduction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, to age 55 and one-half of the early retirement reduction from age 55 to the age the annuity begins.</p>
          <p>(d) The surviving spouse eligible for surviving spouse benefits under this subdivision may apply for the annuity any time after the member's death. The benefit may not begin to accrue more than six months before the date the application is filed with the executive director and may not accrue before the member's death. The benefit is payable for life. Any benefit under this subdivision is in lieu of benefits under subdivision 1, if applicable, and in lieu of a refund of accumulated member contributions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.47#stat.354.47.1">354.47, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p>(e) For purposes of this subdivision, a designated beneficiary must be a former spouse or a biological or adopted child of the member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.46.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.46.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Survivor coverage term certain.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) In lieu of the 100 percent optional annuity under subdivision 2, or a refund under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.47#stat.354.47.1">354.47, subdivision 1</a>, the surviving spouse of a deceased member may elect to receive survivor coverage in a term certain of five, ten, 15, or 20 years, but monthly payments must not exceed 75 percent of the average high-five monthly salary of the deceased member. The monthly term certain annuity must be actuarially equivalent to the 100 percent optional annuity under subdivision 2.</p>
          <p>(b) If a surviving spouse elects a term certain payment and dies before the expiration of the specified term certain period, the commuted value of the remaining annuity payments must be paid in a lump sum to the surviving spouse's estate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.46.2b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.46.2b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Dependent child survivor coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>If there is no surviving spouse eligible for benefits under subdivision 2, each dependent child as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.8a">354.05, subdivision 8a</a>, is eligible for monthly surviving child benefits. Surviving child benefits to a dependent child must be paid from the date of the member's death to the date the dependent child attains age 20 if the child is under age 15 on the date of the member's death. If the child is 15 years or older on the date of the member's death, the surviving child benefit is payable for five years. The dependent surviving child benefit is an amount that is actuarially equivalent to the value of a 100 percent optional annuity under subdivision 2 calculated using the age of the member and age of the dependent child as of the date of death in lieu of the age of the member and the spouse. If there is more than one dependent child, each dependent child is entitled to receive a proportionate share of the actuarial value of the member's account.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.46.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.46.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Payment after death of retiree.</span></h2>
          <p>If a former member dies after retirement, the former member or a designated beneficiary shall be entitled to the annuity payment due for the full month during which death occurs if no optional or reversionary annuity was designated by the member. If an optional or reversionary annuity has been designated by the member, a death benefit shall be paid in accordance with the provisions of such annuity as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.45">354.45</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.46.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.46.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.46.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.46.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Payment to designated beneficiary.</span></h2>
          <p>A member who is single or, if the member is married, a member and the spouse of the member jointly, may make a specification in writing on a form prescribed by the executive director that the benefits provided in subdivision 2, or in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.47#stat.354.47.1">354.47, subdivision 1</a>, must be paid only to a designated beneficiary or to designated beneficiaries. For purposes of subdivision 2, a designated beneficiary may only be either a former spouse or a biological or an adopted child of the member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.46.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.46.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A beneficiary designation and an application for benefits under this section must be in writing on a form prescribed by the executive director.</p>
          <p>(b) Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.55#stat.354.55.11">354.55, subdivision 11</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.311">356.311</a> apply to a deferred annuity payable under this section.</p>
          <p>(c) Unless otherwise specified, the annuity must be computed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44">354.44</a>, subdivision 2 or 6, whichever is applicable.</p>
          <p>(d) Each designated beneficiary eligible for a lifetime benefit under this subdivision may apply for an annuity any time after the member's death. The benefit may not begin to accrue more than six months before the date the application is filed with the executive director and may not accrue before the member's death.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.46.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.46.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A survivor benefit under subdivision 1, 2, 2a, or 2b, is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.46.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
