      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.465"><b>354.465</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.37.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 37</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.47">
        <h1 class="shn">354.47 PAYMENT AFTER DEATH.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.47.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.47.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Death before retirement.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a member dies before retirement and is covered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.2">354.44, subdivision 2</a>, and neither an optional annuity, nor a reversionary annuity, nor a benefit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.46#stat.354.46.1">354.46, subdivision 1</a>, is payable to the survivors if the member was a basic member, then the surviving spouse, or if there is no surviving spouse, the designated beneficiary is entitled to an amount equal to the member's accumulated deductions with interest credited to the account of the member to the date of death of the member. If the designated beneficiary is a minor, interest must be credited to the date the beneficiary reaches legal age, or the date of receipt, whichever is earlier.</p>
          <p>(b) If a member dies before retirement and is covered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, and neither an optional annuity, nor reversionary annuity, nor the benefit described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.46#stat.354.46.1">354.46, subdivision 1</a>, is payable to the survivors if the member was a basic member, then the surviving spouse, or if there is no surviving spouse, then the designated beneficiary is entitled to a refund equal to the accumulated deductions credited to the member's account plus interest compounded annually until the member's date of death using the following interest rates:</p>
          <p>(1) before July 1, 1957, no interest accrues;</p>
          <p>(2) July 1, 1957, to June 30, 2011, six percent; and</p>
          <p>(3) after June 30, 2011, four percent.</p>
          <p>(c) If the designated beneficiary under paragraph (b) is a minor, any interest credited under that paragraph must be credited to the date the beneficiary reaches legal age, or the date of receipt, whichever is earlier.</p>
          <p>(d) The amount of any refund payable under this subdivision must be reduced by any permanent disability payment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.48">354.48</a> received by the member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.47.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.47.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Uncashed annuity or benefit warrants.</span></h2>
          <p>Uncashed annuity or benefit warrants issued before the recipient's death are payable to the designated beneficiary, and if none, to the recipient's estate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.47.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.47.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Benefits of $1,500 or less.</span></h2>
          <p>If a member or a former member dies without having a surviving designated beneficiary and the amount to the credit of the decedent is $1,500 or less, the board of trustees may 90 days after the date of death, in the absence of probate proceedings, make payment to the surviving spouse of the decedent. This payment is a bar to recovery of this payment from the association by any other person or persons. Any accrued retirement annuity, disability, or survivor benefit may be paid in the same manner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.47.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.47.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.47.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/16/#laws.0.9.0">Ex1957 c 16 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/50/#laws.0.48.0">Ex1959 c 50 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/1/17/#laws.0.13.0">Ex1961 c 17 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/845/#laws.0.6.0">1963 c 845 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/821/#laws.0.12.0">1965 c 821 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/485/#laws.0.21.0">1969 c 485 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/40/#laws.0.19.0">1971 c 40 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.32.0">1974 c 289 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/342/#laws.0.13.0">1980 c 342 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/156/#laws.0.4.0">1981 c 156 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.112.0">1981 c 224 s 112</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/578/#laws.3.3.0">1982 c 578 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/564/#laws.0.33.0">1984 c 564 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.2.17.0">1989 c 319 art 2 s 17</a>; art 13 s 63; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.35.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.3.22.0">1994 c 528 art 3 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.36.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.4.25.0">2009 c 169 art 4 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.54.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 54</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.471">
        <h1 class="shn">354.471 ACCOUNT TERMINATION; RESTORATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.471.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.471.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Account termination.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">If an active or deferred member dies and there is no surviving spouse or other beneficiaries, or the spouse or beneficiaries cannot be located within five years of the date of death of the member, the accumulated employee and employer contributions and any other payments made to the Teachers Retirement Association fund by the individual or on behalf of the individual, and all investment earnings on these amounts, must be credited to and become part of the retirement fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.471.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.471.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Restoration.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Following a forfeiture under subdivision 1, if a surviving spouse or other beneficiary of the deceased contacts the Teachers Retirement Association and, based on documentation determined by the executive director to be valid and adequate, establishes a right to a survivor annuity, death refund, or other benefit provided by this chapter, the account forfeited under subdivision 1 must be fully or partially restored, as necessary.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.471.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.39.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 39</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.48">
        <h1 class="shn">354.48 PERMANENT DISABILITY BENEFITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Age and service requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>A member who is totally and permanently disabled, who has not reached the normal retirement age as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.38">354.05, subdivision 38</a>, and who has at least three years of credited allowable service at the time that the total and permanent disability begins is entitled to a disability benefit based on this allowable service in an amount provided in subdivision 3. If the disabled member's teaching service has terminated at any time, at least two of the required three years of allowable service must have been rendered after last becoming a member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Applications; accrual.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person described in subdivision 1, or another person authorized to act on behalf of the person, may make written application on a form prescribed by the executive director for a total and permanent disability benefit only within the 18-month period following the termination of teaching service.</p>
          <p>(b) The benefit accrues from the day following the commencement of the disability or the day following the last day for which salary is paid, whichever is later, but does not begin to accrue more than six months before the date on which the written application is filed with the executive director. If salary is being received for either annual or sick leave during the disability period, the disability benefit accrues from the day following the last day for which this salary is paid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Computation of benefits.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The disability benefit granted to members covered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, must be computed in the same manner as the annuity provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>. The disability benefit is the formula annuity without the reduction for each month the member is under normal retirement age when the benefit begins to accrue as defined by the law in effect on the last day for which salary is paid.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For the purposes of computing a retirement annuity when the member becomes eligible, the amounts paid for disability benefits must not be deducted from the individual member's accumulated deductions. If the disability benefits provided in this subdivision exceed the monthly average salary of the disabled member, the disability benefits must be reduced to an amount equal to the disabled member's average salary.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.3a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.3a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Optional annuity election.</span></h2>
          <p>A disabled member may elect to receive the normal disability benefit or an optional annuity as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.45#stat.354.45.1">354.45, subdivision 1</a>. The election of an optional annuity shall be made prior to commencement of payment of the disability benefit and shall be effective and shall begin to accrue on the same date the disability benefit begins to accrue.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Determination by executive director.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The executive director shall have the member examined by at least two licensed physicians, licensed chiropractors, or licensed psychologists.</p>
          <p>(b) These physicians, chiropractors, APRNs, or psychologists with respect to a mental impairment, shall make written reports to the executive director concerning the member's disability, including expert opinions as to whether or not the member is permanently and totally disabled within the meaning of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.14">354.05, subdivision 14</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) The executive director shall also obtain written certification from the last employer stating whether or not the member was separated from service because of a disability which would reasonably prevent further service to the employer and as a consequence the member is not entitled to compensation from the employer.</p>
          <p>(d) If, upon the consideration of the reports of the physicians, chiropractors, APRNs, or psychologists and any other evidence presented by the member or by others interested therein, the executive director finds that the member is totally and permanently disabled, the executive director shall grant the member a disability benefit.</p>
          <p>(e) An employee who is placed on leave of absence without compensation because of disability is not barred from receiving a disability benefit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.</h2>
          <p>MS 1981 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/578/#laws.3.19.0">1982 c 578 art 3 s 19</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.34.0">1991 c 340 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Regular physical examinations.</span></h2>
          <p>At least once each year during the first five years following the allowance of a disability benefit to any member, and at least once in every three-year period thereafter, the executive director may require the disability benefit recipient to undergo an expert examination by a physician or physicians, by a chiropractor or chiropractors, by an APRN or APRNs, or by one or more psychologists with respect to a mental impairment, engaged by the executive director. If an examination indicates that the member is no longer permanently and totally disabled or that the member is engaged or is able to engage in a substantial gainful occupation, payments of the disability benefit by the association must be discontinued. The payments must be discontinued as soon as the member is reinstated to the payroll following sick leave, but payment may not be made for more than 60 days after the physicians, chiropractors, APRNs, or psychologists engaged by the executive director find that the person is no longer permanently and totally disabled.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.6a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.6a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.<span class="headnote">Medical adviser; duties.</span></h2>
          <p> The executive director may contract with an accredited independent organization specializing in disability determinations, licensed physicians, or physicians on the staff of the commissioner of health as designated by the commissioner, to be the medical adviser to the executive director. The medical adviser shall designate licensed physicians who shall examine applicants for disability benefits. The medical adviser shall pass upon all expert reports based on any examinations performed in order to determine whether a teacher is totally and permanently disabled as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.14">354.05, subdivision 14</a>. The medical adviser shall also investigate all health and medical statements and certificates by or on behalf of a teacher in connection with a disability benefit, and shall report in writing to the director setting forth any conclusions and recommendations on all matters referred to the medical adviser.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Partial reemployment.</span></h2>
          <p>If the disabled person resumes a gainful occupation in which earnings are less than the person's salary at the date of disability, the amount of earnings plus the disability benefit originally granted may not exceed the salary at the date of disability. If the sum of earnings plus the disability benefit originally granted exceeds the salary at the date of disability, the amount of excess earnings must be deducted from the disability benefit payable during the following calendar year. The provisions of this subdivision shall not prohibit the executive director from making a determination that a member is no longer totally and permanently disabled or that the member is engaged or is able to engage in a substantial gainful occupation based on the results of the regular physical examinations required by subdivision 6 or any other physical examinations required by the board. Payment of the disability benefit provided in this subdivision during a period of partial reemployment shall be discontinued if the executive director finds that the member is no longer totally and permanently disabled.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Refusal of examination.</span></h2>
          <p>If any disabled person refuses to submit to a medical examination as required under this section, payments by the association must be discontinued and all rights of the member in any disability benefit must be revoked by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Return to teaching service.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person receiving a disability benefit who is restored to active teaching service shall have deductions taken for the retirement fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Retirement status at normal retirement age.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) No person is entitled to receive both a disability benefit and a retirement annuity provided by this chapter.</p>
          <p>(b) The disability benefit paid to a person hereunder must terminate at the end of the month in which the person attains the normal retirement age. If the person is still totally and permanently disabled at the beginning of the month next following the month in which the person attains the normal retirement age, the person must be deemed to be on retirement status and, if the person had elected an optional annuity under subdivision 3a, must receive an annuity in accordance with the terms of the optional annuity previously elected, or, if the person had not elected an optional annuity under subdivision 3a, may elect to receive a straight life retirement annuity equal to the disability benefit paid before the date on which the person attains the normal retirement age or reaches the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later, or may elect to receive an optional annuity as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.45#stat.354.45.1">354.45, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) Election of an optional annuity must be made within 90 days of the normal retirement age or the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later.</p>
          <p>(d) If an optional annuity is elected, the election is effective on the date on which the person attains the normal retirement age or reaches the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later. The optional annuity begins to accrue on the first day of the month next following the month in which the person attains the normal retirement age or reaches the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.48.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.48.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A disability benefit under this section is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.48.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/16/#laws.0.10.0">Ex1957 c 16 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/50/#laws.0.15.0">Ex1959 c 50 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/597/#laws.0.3.0">1961 c 597 s 3</a>;       <div class="section" id="stat.354.49">
        <h1 class="shn">354.49 REFUND.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.49.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.49.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Entitlement, application.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who ceases to render teaching service in any school or institution to which the provisions of this chapter apply is entitled to a refund provided in subdivision 2, or a deferred retirement annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.55#stat.354.55.11">354.55, subdivision 11</a>. An application for a refund must not be made sooner than 30 days after termination of teaching service if the applicant has not again become a teacher. This payment must be made within 45 days after the receipt of an application for a refund or upon the receipt of member reporting data under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.52#stat.354.52.4a">354.52, subdivision 4a</a>, and payroll cycle data under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.52#stat.354.52.4b">354.52, subdivision 4b</a>, whichever is later.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.49.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.49.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Calculation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.1">354.44, subdivision 1</a>, any person who ceases to be a member by reason of termination of teaching service, is entitled to receive a refund in an amount equal to the accumulated deductions credited to the account plus interest compounded annually using the following interest rates:</p>
          <p>(1) before July 1, 1957, no interest accrues;</p>
          <p>(2) July 1, 1957, to June 30, 2011, six percent;</p>
          <p>(3) July 1, 2011, to June 30, 2018, four percent; and</p>
          <p>(4) after June 30, 2018, three percent.</p>
          <p>For the purpose of this subdivision, interest must be computed on fiscal year end balances through the last day of the month prior to the month in which the refund is issued.</p>
          <p>(b) If the person has received permanent disability payments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.48">354.48</a>, the refund amount must be reduced by the amount of those payments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.49.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.49.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Refund in certain instances.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person not covered by the formula program who has attained normal retirement age with less than the minimum service required for an annuity and who ceases to be a member because of termination of teaching service is entitled upon application to a refund in an amount equal to the person's accumulated deductions plus interest at the rates used to compute annuities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.2">354.44, subdivision 2</a> in lieu of a proportionate annuity pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.32">356.32</a>. If a person who has attained normal retirement age is eligible for an annuity or is covered by the formula program, the refund is an amount equal to the accumulated deductions credited to the person's account as of June 30, 1957, and after July 1, 1957, the accumulated deductions plus interest computed as provided under subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.49.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.49.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Termination of membership.</span></h2>
          <p>Membership in the retirement association of any person shall terminate under the following conditions: (1) if a person takes a refund pursuant to this section; (2) when a person's retirement annuity first begins to accrue as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44">354.44</a>, subdivisions 1 and 4; (3) upon the person's ceasing to be a "teacher" whether by resignation, dismissal, or termination of temporary or provisional employment for the purpose of determining eligibility for disability or survivorship benefits as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.48#stat.354.48.1">354.48, subdivision 1</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.46#stat.354.46.1">354.46, subdivision 1</a>, respectively.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.49.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.49.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.50.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 50</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.49.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/16/#laws.0.11.0">Ex1957 c 16 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/50/#laws.0.16.0">Ex1959 c 50 s 16</a>,17; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/372/#laws.9.27.0">1987 c 372 art 9 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.13.67.0">1989 c 319 art 13 s 67</a>,68; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.37.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 37</a>,38; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.26.0">1991 c 340 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.3.24.0">1994 c 528 art 3 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.38.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.8.14.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 8 s 14</a>,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.8.6.0">2020 c 108 art 8 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.50">
        <h1 class="shn">354.50 TERMINATION OF SERVICE CREDITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.50.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.50.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Repayment of refund.</span></h2>
          <p>When any member accepts a refund provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.49">354.49</a>, all existing service credits to which the member was entitled before accepting the refund terminate and are not restored until the former member acquires not less than two years allowable service credit after taking the last refund. In that event the former member may repay the refund. If more than one refund has been taken, all refunds must be repaid. Teaching service covered under the provisions of chapter 354B may be used to fulfill the two-year requirement of this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.50.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.50.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Interest charge.</span></h2>
          <p>If a member desires to repay the refunds, payment shall include interest at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.4">356.59, subdivision 4</a>, compounded annually, from date of withdrawal to the date payment is made and shall be credited to the fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.50.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.50.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.50.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.50.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Repayment of refund.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person who has received a refund from the Teachers Retirement Association and who is a member of any public retirement fund referred to in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.311">356.311</a> may repay such refund with interest to the Teachers Retirement Association. Repayment of the refunds under the provisions of this subdivision will be in accordance with all the provisions of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.50.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.50.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Refund of variable account accumulations.</span></h2>
          <p>A member who received a refund of variable account accumulations may repay this refund to the member's formula account under this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.50.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/16/#laws.0.12.0">Ex1957 c 16 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/1/17/#laws.0.14.0">Ex1961 c 17 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/6/#laws.0.4.0">Ex1967 c 6 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/485/#laws.0.25.0">1969 c 485 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/40/#laws.0.23.0">1971 c 40 s 23</a>,24; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/728/#laws.0.22.0">1973 c 728 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.39.0">1974 c 289 s 39</a>-41; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/67/#laws.0.10.0">1977 c 67 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.115.0">1981 c 224 s 115</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.9.2.0">1989 c 319 art 9 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.39.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.27.0">1991 c 340 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/598/#laws.3.3.0">1992 c 598 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.25.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/50/#laws.1.114.0">2019 c 50 art 1 s 114</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.51">
        <h1 class="shn">354.51 PAYMENTS TO RECEIVE CREDIT FOR PRIOR SERVICE; SHORTAGES IN MEMBER DEDUCTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.51.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.51.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility to make payments.</span></h2>
          <p>No member is entitled to make payments in lieu of salary deductions to the retirement board to receive allowable service credit for any period of service rendered before that date for which employee contributions were not deducted from the member's salary, except as provided in subdivision 5, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.50">354.50</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.53">354.53</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.51.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.51.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.51.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.51.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.51.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.51.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.37.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 37</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.51.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.51.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Payment of shortages.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), in the event that full required member contributions are not deducted from the salary of a teacher, payment of shortages in member deductions on salary earned are the sole obligation of the employing unit and are payable by the employing unit upon notification by the executive director of the shortage. The amount of the shortage shall be paid with interest at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.4">356.59, subdivision 4</a>, compounded annually, from the end of the fiscal year in which the shortage occurred to the end of the month in which payment is made and the interest must be credited to the fund. The employing unit shall also pay the employer contributions as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.42">354.42</a>, subdivisions 3 and 5 for the shortages. If the shortage payment is not paid by the employing unit within 60 days of notification, and if the executive director does not use the recovery procedure in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.512">354.512</a>, the executive director shall certify the amount of the shortage to the applicable county auditor, who shall spread a levy in the amount of the shortage payment over the taxable property of the taxing district of the employing unit if the employing unit is supported by property taxes. Payment may not be made for shortages in member deductions on salary paid or payable under paragraph (b) or for shortages in member deductions for persons employed by the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system in a faculty position or in an eligible unclassified administrative position and whose employment was less than 25 percent of a full academic year, exclusive of the summer session, for the applicable institution that exceeds the most recent 36 months.</p>
          <p>(b) For a person who is employed by the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system in a faculty position or in an eligible unclassified administrative position and whose employment was less than 25 percent of a full academic year, exclusive of the summer session, for the applicable institution, upon the person's election under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354B.211">354B.211</a> of retirement coverage under this chapter, the shortage in member deductions on the salary for employment by the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system institution of less than 25 percent of a full academic year, exclusive of the summer session, for the applicable institution for the most recent 36 months and the associated employer contributions must be paid by the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system institution, plus interest at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.4">356.59, subdivision 4</a>, compounded annually, from the end of the fiscal year in which the shortage occurred to the end of the month in which the Teachers Retirement Association coverage election is made. An individual electing coverage under this paragraph shall repay the amount of the shortage in member deductions, plus interest, through deduction from salary or compensation payments within the first year of employment after the election under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354B.211">354B.211</a>, subject to the limitations in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16D.16">16D.16</a>. The Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system may use any means available to recover amounts which were not recovered through deductions from salary or compensation payments. No payment of the shortage in member deductions under this paragraph may be made for a period longer than the most recent 36 months.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.51.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/16/#laws.0.13.0">Ex1957 c 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/50/#laws.0.18.0">Ex1959 c 50 s 18</a>,19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/485/#laws.0.26.0">1969 c 485 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/40/#laws.0.25.0">1971 c 40 s 25</a>,26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.42.0">1974 c 289 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/160/#laws.0.11.0">1981 c 160 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.116.0">1981 c 224 s 116</a>-118; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/578/#laws.3.19.0">1982 c 578 art 3 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/12/#laws.11.7.0">1Sp1985 c 12 art 11 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/284/#laws.6.9.0">1987 c 284 art 6 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/598/#laws.3.4.0">1992 c 598 art 3 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.5.2.0">2004 c 267 art 5 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/286/#laws.8.4.0">2012 c 286 art 8 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.36.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 36</a>,37; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.26.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.5.4.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 5 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.511"><b>354.511</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.512">
        <h1 class="shn">354.512 RECOVERY OF DEFICIENCIES.</h1>
        <p>In addition to any other remedies permitted under law, if an employing unit or other entity required by law to make any form of payment to the Teachers Retirement Association fails to make full payment, the executive director is authorized to certify the amount of deficiency to the commissioner of management and budget, who shall deduct the amount from any state aid or appropriation applicable to the employing unit or entity, and transmit the withheld aid or appropriation to the executive director for deposit in the fund.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.512.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/286/#laws.8.5.0">2012 c 286 art 8 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.11.11.0">2018 c 211 art 11 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.52">
        <h1 class="shn">354.52 REPORTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.47.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 47</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote"> Postretirement income reporting.</span></h2>
          <p>Each employing unit must report to the executive director the amount of income earned during the previous fiscal year by each retiree for teaching service performed after retirement. This shall be done through the payroll reporting system and is based on reemployment income as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.5">354.44, subdivision 5</a>. Submitting the salary data through payroll reporting has the force and effect of an oath as to the correctness of the amount of postretirement reemployment income earned.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Deduction requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>Every pay period, each employer shall deduct and withhold from the salary of every teacher who is a member of the fund the amount required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.42">354.42</a>. At the time of each deduction, the employer shall also furnish to each teacher a statement showing the amount of the deduction.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Reporting and remittance requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>An employer shall remit all amounts due to the association and furnish a statement indicating the amount due and transmitted with any other information required by the executive director. If an amount due is not received by the association within 14 calendar days of the payroll payment, the employer shall pay interest on the amount due at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.4">356.59, subdivision 4</a>, compounded annually, from the due date until the amount is received by the association. All amounts due and other employer obligations not remitted may be certified to the commissioner of management and budget who shall deduct the amount from any state aid or appropriation amount applicable to the employing unit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Member data reporting requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An employing unit must provide the member data specified in paragraph (b) in a format prescribed by the executive director. Data changes and the dates of those changes under this subdivision must be reported to the association in a format prescribed by the executive director on an ongoing basis within 14 calendar days after the date of the end of the payroll cycle in which they occur. These data changes must be reported with the payroll cycle data under subdivision 4b.</p>
          <p>(b) Data on the member includes:</p>
          <p>(1) legal name, address, date of birth, association member number, employer-assigned employee number, and Social Security number;</p>
          <p>(2) association status, including, but not limited to, basic, coordinated, exempt annuitant, exempt technical college teacher, and exempt independent contractor or consultant;</p>
          <p>(3) employment status, including, but not limited to, full time, part time, intermittent, substitute, or part-time mobility;</p>
          <p>(4) employment position, including, but not limited to, teacher, superintendent, principal, administrator, or other;</p>
          <p>(5) employment activity, including, but not limited to, hire, termination, resumption of employment, disability, or death;</p>
          <p>(6) leaves of absence;</p>
          <p>(7) county district number assigned by the association for the employing unit;</p>
          <p>(8) data center identification number, if applicable;</p>
          <p>(9) gender;</p>
          <p>(10) position code; and</p>
          <p>(11) other information as may be required by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.4b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.4b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Payroll cycle reporting requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>An employing unit shall provide the following data to the association for payroll payments on an ongoing basis within 14 calendar days after the date of the payroll payment in a format prescribed by the executive director:</p>
          <p>(1) association member number;</p>
          <p>(2) employer-assigned employee number;</p>
          <p>(3) Social Security number;</p>
          <p>(4) amount of each salary deduction;</p>
          <p>(5) amount of salary as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.35">354.05, subdivision 35</a>, from which each deduction was made;</p>
          <p>(6) reason for payment;</p>
          <p>(7) the beginning and ending dates of the payroll period covered and the date of actual payment;</p>
          <p>(8) fiscal year of salary earnings;</p>
          <p>(9) total remittance amount including employee, employer, and additional employer contributions;</p>
          <p>(10) reemployed annuitant salary under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.5">354.44, subdivision 5</a>; and</p>
          <p>(11) other information as may be required by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.4c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.4c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4c.<span class="headnote">MnSCU service credit reporting.</span></h2>
          <p>For all part-time service rendered, the service credit reporting requirement in subdivision 4b for all part-time employees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system must be met by the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system reporting to the association on or before July 31 of each year the final calculation of each part-time member's service credit for the immediately preceding fiscal year based on the employee's assignments for the fiscal year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.4d">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4d." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.4d">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4d.<span class="headnote">Annual base salary reporting.</span></h2>
          <p>An employing unit must provide annual base salary data, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.41">354.05, subdivision 41</a>, to the association on or before June 30 of each fiscal year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Liability for moneys.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of management and budget, the several county treasurers, and the treasurers of the various school districts and institutions to which the provisions of this chapter apply shall be officially liable for the receipt, handling, and disbursement of all moneys coming into their hands belonging to the fund and the sureties on the official bonds of each of these treasurers and the commissioner of management and budget shall be liable for such moneys the same as for all other moneys belonging to the school funds of this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.52.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.52.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Noncompliance consequences.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An employing unit that does not comply with the reporting requirements under subdivision 2a, 4a, 4b, or 4d, must pay a fine of $5 per calendar day until the association receives the required data.</p>
          <p>(b) If the annual base salary required to be reported under subdivision 4d has not been settled or determined as of June 16, the fine commences if the annual base salary has not been reported to the association within 14 days following the settlement date.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.52.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/16/#laws.0.16.0">Ex1957 c 16 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/584/#laws.0.1.0">1963 c 584 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/447/#laws.0.1.0">1965 c 447 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/485/#laws.0.27.0">1969 c 485 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.43.0">1974 c 289 s 43</a>-45; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/160/#laws.0.12.0">1981 c 160 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.40.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 40</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/340/#laws.0.28.0">1991 c 340 s 28</a>,29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/598/#laws.3.6.0">1992 c 598 art 3 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.3.25.0">1994 c 528 art 3 s 25</a>-30; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/141/#laws.3.11.0">1995 c 141 art 3 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.39.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 39</a>-42; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/10/#laws.3.18.0">1Sp2001 c 10 art 3 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.42.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.6.2.0">2004 c 267 art 6 s 2</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.38.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.27.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 27</a>; art 11 s 12,13;       <div class="section" id="stat.354.53">
        <h1 class="shn">354.53 CREDIT FOR BREAK IN SERVICE TO PROVIDE UNIFORMED SERVICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.53.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.53.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility; employee and employer contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any teacher who is absent from employment by reason of service in the uniformed services, as defined in United States Code, title 38, section 4303(13), and who returns to the employer providing teaching service upon discharge from service in the uniformed service within the time frames required in United States Code, title 38, section 4312(e), may obtain service credit for the period of the uniformed service as further specified in this section, provided that the teacher did not separate from uniformed service with a dishonorable or bad conduct discharge or under other than honorable conditions.</p>
          <p>(b) The member may obtain credit by paying into the fund an equivalent employee contribution based upon the contribution rate or rates in effect at the time that the uniformed service was performed multiplied by the full and fractional years being purchased and applied to the annual salary rate. The annual salary rate is the average annual salary during the purchase period that the teacher would have received if the teacher had continued to provide teaching service to the employer rather than provide uniformed service or if the determination of that rate is not reasonably certain, the annual salary rate is the teacher's average salary rate during the 12-month period immediately preceding the period, or, if the preceding period is less than 12 months, the annualized rate derived from the teacher's average salary rate during the period of teacher employment rendered immediately preceding the period of the uniformed service.</p>
          <p>(c) The equivalent employer contribution and, if applicable, the equivalent additional contribution provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.42">354.42</a> must be paid by the employing unit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.52#stat.354.52.4">354.52, subdivision 4</a>, using the employer and employer additional contribution rate or rates in effect at the time that the uniformed service was performed, applied to the same annual salary rate or rates used to compute the equivalent employee contribution.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.53.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.53.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Calculation of credit.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of computing a money purchase annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.2">354.44, subdivision 2</a>, all payments into the fund under this section must be considered accumulations after July 1, 1957.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of computing a formula annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, if the employee equivalent contributions provided in this section are not paid in full, the member's formula service credit must be prorated by multiplying the full and fractional number of years of uniformed service eligible for purchase by the ratio obtained by dividing the total employee contribution received by the total employee contribution otherwise required under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.53.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.53.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Eligible payment period.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) To receive service credit under this section, the contributions specified in this section must be transmitted to the Teachers Retirement Association during the period which begins with the date on which the individual returns to teaching service and which has a duration of three times the length of the uniformed service period, but not to exceed five years.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), if the payment period determined under paragraph (a) is less than three years, the contributions required under this section to receive service credit may be made within three years from the discharge date.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.53.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.53.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Limits on service credit.</span></h2>
          <p>The amount of service credit obtainable under this section may not exceed five years, unless a longer purchase period is required under United States Code, title 38, section 4312.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.53.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.53.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Interest requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The employer shall pay interest on all equivalent employee and employer contribution amounts payable under this section at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.4">356.59, subdivision 4</a>, compounded annually, from the end of each fiscal year of the leave or the break in service to the end of the month in which the payment is received.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.53.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/1/16/#laws.0.17.0">Ex1957 c 16 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/50/#laws.0.20.0">Ex1959 c 50 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/821/#laws.0.13.0">1965 c 821 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/834/#laws.0.7.0">1967 c 834 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/40/#laws.0.27.0">1971 c 40 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/270/#laws.0.12.0">1973 c 270 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.46.0">1974 c 289 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/306/#laws.0.23.0">1975 c 306 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/67/#laws.0.11.0">1977 c 67 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/97/#laws.0.4.0">1977 c 97 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.122.0">1981 c 224 s 122</a>,123; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/12/#laws.11.8.0">1Sp1985 c 12 art 11 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/598/#laws.3.7.0">1992 c 598 art 3 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/305/#laws.1.81.0">1996 c 305 art 1 s 81</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/233/#laws.1.53.0">1997 c 233 art 1 s 53</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.16.17.0">1999 c 222 art 16 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.3.6.0">2004 c 267 art 3 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.28.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/47/#laws.8.1.0">2023 c 47 art 8 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.531"><b>354.531</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.2.28.0">1989 c 319 art 2 s 28</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.532"><b>354.532</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.2.28.0">1989 c 319 art 2 s 28</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.533"><b>354.533</b> MS 2006 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.16.16.0">1999 c 222 art 16 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.7.1.0">2002 c 392 art 7 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/12/#laws.6.2.0">1Sp2003 c 12 art 6 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.17.6.0">2004 c 267 art 17 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.2.3.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 2 s 3</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.534"><b>354.534</b> MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.16.16.0">1999 c 222 art 16 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.7.1.0">2002 c 392 art 7 s 1</a>; 1Sp2003 c 12 art 6 s 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.17.6.0">2004 c 267 art 17 s 6</a>]</div>

      <div class="sr" id="stat.354.542"><b>354.542</b> MS 2002 [Expired]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.543">
        <h1 class="shn">354.543 PRIOR OR UNCREDITED MILITARY SERVICE CREDIT PURCHASE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.543.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.543.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Service credit purchase authorized.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If paragraph (b) does not apply, a teacher who has at least three years of allowable service credit with the Teachers Retirement Association and who performed service in the United States armed forces before becoming a teacher as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.2">354.05, subdivision 2</a>, or who failed to obtain service credit for a military leave of absence under the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.53">354.53</a>, is entitled to purchase allowable and formula service credit for the initial period of enlistment, induction, or call to active duty without any voluntary extension by making payment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.551">356.551</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A service credit purchase is prohibited if:</p>
          <p>(1) the teacher separated from service with the United States armed forces with a dishonorable or bad conduct discharge or under other than honorable conditions; or</p>
          <p>(2) the teacher has purchased or otherwise received service credit from any Minnesota defined benefit public employee pension plan, other than a volunteer fire plan, for the same period of service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.543.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.543.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application and documentation.</span></h2>
          <p>A teacher who desires to purchase service credit under subdivision 1 must apply with the executive director to make the purchase. The application must include all necessary documentation of the teacher's qualifications to make the purchase, signed written permission to allow the executive director to request and receive necessary verification of applicable facts and eligibility requirements, and any other relevant information that the executive director may require.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.543.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.543.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Service credit grant.</span></h2>
          <p>Allowable and formula service credit for the purchase period must be granted by the Teachers Retirement Association to the purchasing teacher upon receipt of the purchase payment amount. Payment must be made before the teacher's termination of teaching service. Purchasing allowable and formula service credit under this section does not change the date the teacher first became a member of the association for the purpose of computing an annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.543.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.4.31.0">2009 c 169 art 4 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.8.7.0">2020 c 108 art 8 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.544">
        <h1 class="shn">354.544 SERVICE CREDIT PURCHASE FOR TEACHING SERVICE IN OTHER STATES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.544.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.544.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Service credit purchase authorized.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A teacher who has at least three years of allowable service credit with the Teachers Retirement Association is entitled to purchase up to five years of allowable and formula service credit for periods of service as an elementary or secondary public or charter school teacher in another state by making payment of the purchase payment amount under subdivision 4.</p>
          <p>(b) Periods of service that may be purchased are:</p>
          <p>(1) periods of service as a teacher in another state before becoming a member of the association; and</p>
          <p>(2) periods of service as a teacher in another state during any period of employment after terminating teaching service as a member of the association and before returning to teaching service as a member of the association.</p>
          <p>(c) A teacher is not permitted to purchase a period of service if the purchase would result in an entitlement to service credit for the same period of service in more than one pension or retirement plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.544.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.544.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application and documentation.</span></h2>
          <p>A teacher who desires to purchase service credit under subdivision 1 must apply with the executive director to make the purchase. The application must include:</p>
          <p>(1) documentation showing the teacher's period of employment as a teacher in another state;</p>
          <p>(2) the period of employment for which service credit is being purchased;</p>
          <p>(3) permission to allow the executive director to request and receive verification of such employment;</p>
          <p>(4) documentation showing that the teacher has forfeited all rights to benefits for the period of employment as a teacher in another state; and</p>
          <p>(5) any other relevant information requested by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.544.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.544.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Grant of service credit.</span></h2>
          <p>The Teachers Retirement Association must grant allowable service credit to the teacher making the purchase for the period being purchased upon receipt of the purchase payment amount. Payment must be made by the earlier of the teacher's termination of teaching service or one year after the executive director's approval of the teacher's application to purchase service credit. Purchasing allowable service credit under this section does not change the date the teacher first became a member of the association for the purpose of computing an annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.544.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.544.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Purchase payment amount.</span></h2>
          <p>The purchase payment amount is the amount determined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.551">356.551</a> for the period or periods of service requested, except that, for purposes of calculating the purchase payment amount to purchase allowable service credit for service as a teacher in another state that occurred before becoming a member of the association or between periods of teaching service as a member of the association, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.551#stat.356.551.2">356.551, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (c), shall not apply.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.354.544.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/65/#laws.8.1.0">2022 c 65 art 8 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.354.55">
        <h1 class="shn">354.55 OPTIONS TO CERTAIN MEMBERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.55.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.55.1">§</a>

        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.55.8">

        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.55.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.55.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/289/#laws.0.59.0">1974 c 289 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.55.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.55.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.8.9.0">2020 c 108 art 8 s 9</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.55.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.55.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Deferred annuity; augmentation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any person covered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, who ceases to render teaching service, may leave the person's accumulated deductions in the fund for the purpose of receiving a deferred annuity at retirement.</p>
          <p>(b) The deferred retirement annuity of any former member must be augmented from the first day of the month following the termination of active service to the effective date of retirement.</p>
          <p>(c) No augmentation is creditable if the deferral period is less than three months or if deferral commenced before July 1, 1971.</p>
          <p>(d) For persons who became covered employees before July 1, 2006, the annuity must be augmented at the following rate or rates, compounded annually:</p>
          <p>(1) five percent until January 1, 1981;</p>
          <p>(2) three percent from January 1, 1981, until January 1 of the year following the year in which the deferred annuitant attains age 55 or June 30, 2012, whichever is earlier;</p>
          <p>(3) five percent from the date established in clause (2) until June 30, 2012;</p>
          <p>(4) two percent from July 1, 2012, until June 30, 2019; and</p>
          <p>(5) after June 30, 2019, the deferred annuity must not be augmented.</p>
          <p>(e) For persons who become covered employees after June 30, 2006, the annuity must be augmented at the following rate or rates, compounded annually:</p>
          <p>(1) 2.5 percent until June 30, 2012;</p>
          <p>(2) two percent from July 1, 2012, until June 30, 2019; and</p>
          <p>(3) after June 30, 2019, the deferred annuity must not be augmented.</p>
          <p>(f) In no case may the annuity payable under this subdivision be less than the amount of annuity payable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
          <p>(g) The requirements and provisions for retirement before normal retirement age contained in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, also apply to an employee fulfilling the requirements with a combination of service as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.311">356.311</a>.</p>
          <p>(h) The augmentation provided by this subdivision applies to the benefit provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.46#stat.354.46.2">354.46, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(i) The augmentation provided by this subdivision does not apply to any period in which a person is on an approved leave of absence from an employer unit covered by the provisions of this chapter.</p>
          <p>(j) The retirement annuity or disability benefit of, or the survivor benefit payable on behalf of, a former teacher who terminated service before July 1, 1997, which is not first payable until after June 30, 1997, must be increased on an actuarial equivalent basis to reflect the change in the investment return actuarial assumption under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>, from five percent to six percent under a calculation procedure and tables adopted by the board as recommended by an approved actuary and approved by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.354.55.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/354/full#stat.354.55.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.37.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 37</a>]</p>
        </div>
